CREATE OR REPLACE Package Body IAS_Gen_Pkg as
Function order_by_name(p_icode In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_CODE%TYPE) return VARCHAR2 Is
cursor nam is select i_name From Ias_Itm_Mst
where i_code=p_icode ;
rslt V
ARCHAR2(100):='';
Begin
open nam ;
fetch nam into rslt ;
close nam;
return rslt;
exception
when others then
return ('');
close nam ;
End ;
--======================================================================================
FUNCTION LF(p_curr In VARCHAR2 ) RETURN NUMBER Is
L NUMBER;
Select l_f Into L From Ex_Rate Where cur_code = p_curr ;
Return L ;
Exception
When Others then
return Null;
END LF;
FUNCTION Get_Cnt (SqlStr VARCHAR2) RETURN NUMBER IS
Val NUMBER;
Execute Immediate SqlStr Into Val;
RETURN Val;
When No_Data_Found Then
RETURN 0;
When Others Then
Raise_ApplICation_Error(-20406,'Error Get_Cnt ' || '' || SqlErrm);
End Get_Cnt;
--===========================================================================================
FUNCTION Get_CurNo (P_Acy In Varchar2) RETURN NUMBER Is
v_CurNo Number;
Select Cur_no
Into v_CurNo
From Ex_Rate
Where Cur_Code=P_Acy;
Return(v_CurNo);
Return(Null);
End Get_CurNo;
FUNCTION Get_Frst_Day RETURN DATE Is
v_frst_day Date;
Select Min(F_date) --to_char(Min(From_date),'DD/MM/YYYY')
Into v_frst_day
From S_PRD_DTL;
Return(v_frst_day);
Select F_date
From S_PRD_DTL
where PRD_NO=1;
End;
End Get_Frst_Day;
--========================================================================================
FUNCTION Get_Final_Day RETURN DATE Is
v_fnal_day Date;
Select Max(T_date) --to_char(Min(From_date),'DD/MM/YYYY')
Into v_fnal_day
Return(v_fnal_day);
Select T_date
where PRD_NO=( Select max(PRD_NO) From S_PRD_DTL );
End Get_Final_Day;
FUNCTION Get_Sysdate RETURN DATE Is
S_SysDate Date;
Select Sysdate
Into S_SysDate
From S_PRD_MST
Where to_char(Sysdate,'YYYY') Between F_yr_no and T_yr_no ;
Return(S_SysDate);
Where to_char(Sysdate,'DD/MM/YYYY') Between F_DATE and T_DATE ;
Select Max(t_date) Into S_SysDate From S_PRD_DTL;
End Get_Sysdate;
FUNCTION Get_Curdate RETURN DATE Is
Select Sysdate Into S_SysDate From dual;
Exception When Others Then
End Get_Curdate;

From Ex_rate
Where L_F=1;
Return(V_Local_cur);
End Get_Local_Cur;

Return(V_stk_cur);
End Get_Stk_Cur;

P_Date In Date Default Null ) Return Number Is
V_Cur_Rate Number ;
V_Up_Date Date ;

FUNCTION Get_Lev_Prv (P_UserNo In Number,P_Icode Varchar2 Default Null,P_Price_Type Number Default Null,P_Wcode Number Default Null,P_Brn_No Number Default Null) RETURN NUMBER Is
V_LevNo Number;
BEGIN
If P_Icode Is Null Then
SELECT Lev_No
Into v_LevNo
From Ias_Priv_Price
Where U_Id = P_UserNo
and Nvl(Add_Flag,0)=1
and Rownum <=1
ORDER BY Lev_No ;
Else
If Nvl(P_Price_Type,1) Not In (2,5) Or (Nvl(P_Price_Type,1)=2 And P_Wcode Is Null) Then
SELECT Ias_Pricing_Levels.Lev_No
From Ias_Pricing_Levels,Ias_Priv_Price
Where Ias_Pricing_Levels.Lev_No=Ias_Priv_Price.Lev_No
and U_Id = P_UserNo
and exists(select 1 From ias_item_price
where lev_no = Ias_Pricing_Levels.lev_no
and i_code = p_icode
and rownum <= 1)
ORDER BY Ias_Pricing_Levels.Lev_No ;
Return(v_LevNo);
When others then
Return (0);
End Get_Lev_Prv;
--===========================================================================

v_LevNo Number;
Where Nvl(Ias_Pricing_Levels.Lev_Deflt,0)=1
and Ias_Pricing_Levels.Lev_No=Ias_Priv_Price.Lev_No
End Get_Lev_Dflt_Prv;
FUNCTION Get_Frm_Prv (P_UserNo In Number , P_Frm_no In Number) RETURN NUMBER Is
v_IncFlg Number;
Select nvl(Include_Flag,0)
Into v_IncFlg
From Privilege
and Form_no = P_Frm_No
and Rownum <=1 ;
Return(v_IncFl
End Get_Frm_Prv;
FUNCTION Get_One_Wcode RETURN NUMBER Is

cnt Number;

Return(v_Wcode);

End Get_One_Wcode;
FUNCTION Get_Itm_Price_DocAcy ( P_Icode In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_CODE%TYPE,
P_Price_Type In Number default 1 ) RETURN NUMBER Is
v_price Number;
Select sum(decode(a_cy,P_acy , i_price,
P_Stk_cur , ((i_price*P_Stk_Rate)/P_ac_rate),
P_Loc_cur , (i_price/P_ac_rate),
((i_price * cur_rate ) / P_ac_rate)))
Into v_price
From Ias_pricing_levels,Ias_Item_Price,Ex_rate
Where Ias_pricing_levels.lev_no=Ias_Item_Price.lev_no
and Ias_Item_Price.lev_no = P_Lev_no
and i_code = P_Icode
and Ias_Item_Price.Itm_Unt = P_Itm_Unt
and cur_code= a_cy
And Decode(P_Price_Type,2,ias_item_price.w_code,0)=Decode(P_Price_Type,2,P_WCode,0)
And Nvl(ias_pricing_levels.Bill_Doc_type,0) = Decode(ias_pricing_levels.Bill_Doc_type,Null,0,
Decode(P_Bill_Doc_type,4,2,1))
And Decode(P_Price_Type,3,Nvl(P_Iqty,From_Qty),4,nvl(P_Iqty,From_Qty),0) Between
Decode(P_Price_Type,3,Nvl(From_Qty,0),4,Nvl(From_Qty,0),0) And Decode(P_Price_Type,3,Nvl(To_Qty,0),4,Nvl(To_Qty,0),0)
And RowNum<=1;
Return(v_price);
When others Then
Return(0);
FUNCTION Get_Low_Price ( P_Icode In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_CODE%TYPE,
P_QTY IN NUMBER DEFAULT NULL ) RETURN NUMBER IS
v_LowPr Number;
v_LowPr_Cur Varchar2(7);
v_LowPr_rate Number;
v_Rate Number;
Select (nvl(i_price,0)),a_cy,nvl(cur_rate,1)
Into v_lowpr,v_LowPr_Cur,v_LowPr_rate
and lev_Low = 1
and Ias_Item_Price.Itm_Unt =P_Itm_Unt
And Decode(P_Price_Type,2,Ias_Item_Price.W_Code,4,Ias_Item_Price.W_Code,0)=Decode(P_Price_Type,2,P_W_Code,4,P_W_Code,0)
And Decode(P_Price_Type,3,Nvl(P_Qty,0),4,Nvl(P_Qty,0),0) Between
Decode(P_Price_Type,3,Nvl(From_Qty,0),4,Nvl(From_Qty,0),0) And Decode(P_Price_Type,3,Nvl(To_Qty,0),4,Nvl(To_Qty,0),0);
--When no_data_found Then
-- v_lowpr:=0;
v_lowpr:=0;
If v_LowPr_Cur = P_acy Then --(1)
v_price:=v_lowpr;
Elsif v_LowPr_Cur = P_Stk_cur Then --(1)
v_price:=(v_lowpr*P_Stk_Rate)/P_ac_rate;
Elsif v_LowPr_Cur = P_Loc_cur Then --(1)
v_price:=v_lowpr/P_ac_rate;
Else--(1)
v_price:=(v_lowpr * v_LowPr_rate ) / P_ac_rate;
End If;--(1)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FUNCTION Get_High_Price ( P_Icode In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_CODE%TYPE,
P_ITM_UNT In Ias_Itm_DTL.ITM_UNT%TYPE,
P_QTY IN NUMBER DEFAULT NULL ) RETURN NUMBER is
v_HighPr Number;
v_HighPr_Cur Varchar2(7);
v_HighPr_rate Number;
v_Rate
Number;
Into v_Highpr,v_HighPr_Cur,v_HighPr_rate
and lev_High = 1
And Ias_Item_Price.Itm_Unt = P_Itm_Unt
and cur_code = a_cy
-- v_Highpr:=0;
v_Highpr:=0;
If v_HighPr_Cur = P_acy Then --(1)
v_price:=v_Highpr;
Elsif v_HighPr_Cur = P_Stk_cur Then --(1)
v_price:=(v_Highpr*P_Stk_Rate)/P_ac_rate;
Elsif v_HighPr_Cur = P_Loc_cur Then --(1)
v_price:=v_Highpr/P_ac_rate;
v_price:=(v_Highpr * v_HighPr_rate ) / P_ac_rate;
----------------------------------------------
--=========================================================================
---## Get_Item_Prcie
FUNCTION Get_Itm_Price (P_LevNo In Number,
P_Frc_no In Number Default 2 ) Return Number Is
v_PriceItem NUMBER;
v_RatePrice NUMBER;
v_CPL NUMBER :=0;
v_Lev_Num NUMBER (2);
v_LevCur varchar2 (7);
If P_LevNo Is Null Then
Select Min(Lev_No)
Into v_Lev_Num
From IAS_PRICING_LEVELS;
Return(null);
v_Lev_Num:=P_LevNo;
Select A_Cy
Into v_LevCur
From IAS_PRICING_LEVELS
Where Lev_no=v_Lev_Num;
Null;
If v_levCur = P_acy and P_billrate Is Not Null Then
v_RatePrice:=P_billrate;
v_RatePrice:=Ias_gen_Pkg.Get_cur_rate(v_LevCur);
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
--BillDocType=4 and
If P_c_code Is Not Null Then --(1)
Select Lev_No
Into v_CPL
From Customer_Curr
Where c_code = P_c_code
and a_cy = P_acy;
If v_CPL Is Not Null Then --(2)
If nvl(v_PriceItem,0) = 0 Then --(3)
Select Round((nvl(I_Price,0)* v_RatePrice * 1/P_BillRate),P_Frc_no)
Into v_PriceItem
From Ias_Item_Price
Where Lev_No =
v_CPL
and I_code = P_Icode;
End If; --(3)
End If; --(2)
END ;
End If; --(1)
If nvl(v_PriceItem,0) = 0 Then --(4)
Select Round((Nvl(I_Price,0) * v_RatePrice * 1/P_BillRate),P_Frc_no)
Where Lev_No = v_Lev_Num
and I_code = P_Icode
and Itm_Unt = P_Itm_Unt
and nvl(w_code,0) = Decode(P_w_code,null,nvl(w_code,0),P_w_code);
null;
End If; --(4)
Return(v_PriceItem );
return(0);
END Get_Itm_Price;
PROCEDURE Get_Fields (P_IAS_User In varchar2, P_Tab_Nm In Varchar2 ,P_WHR In varchar2 Default Null) Is
v_col Varchar2(4000);
Cursor F_cv Is Select COLUMN_NAME From ALL_TAB
_COLUMNS
Where OWNER=P_IAS_User And Table_Name = Upper(P_Tab_Nm) ORDER BY COLUMN_ID;
For i In F_cv Loop
v_col:= v_col||','||i.COLUMN_NAME;
End Loop;
v_col := Substr(v_col,2);
Execute Immediate ('Insert Into '||P_Tab_Nm||' ('||v_col||')
Select '||v_col||' From '||P_IAS_User||'.'||P_Tab_Nm||' '||P_WHR);
Raise_Application_Error(-20408,' Error When Insert Data = '||P_Tab_Nm||' '||Sqlerrm) ;
End Get_Fields;
FUNCTION Get_Fld_Value (P_Tab_Nm In Varchar2 ,P_Fld_Nm In Varchar2,P_WHR In varchar2 Default Null) RETURN VARCHAR2 Is
V_Fld_Value Varchar2(60);
Execute Immediate ('Select '||P_Fld_Nm ||' From '|| P_Tab_Nm ||' '||P_WHR) Into V_Fld_Value ;
Return (V_Fld_Value);
Return Null;
End Get_Fld_Value;
FUNCTION Get_Flg_Nm (P_flg_code In varchar2,P_flg_value In Number ,P_Lng_no In Number Default 1) RETURN VARCHAR2 Is

and flg_code=P_flg_code
and flg_value=P_flg_value
and RowNum<=1;
Return(v_flg_nm);
End Get_Flg_Nm;

END Get_Prompt;

END Get_Msg;

P_Fld_Nm In Varchar2) RETURN NUMBER Is
v_Appr Number;
Select 1
Into v_Appr
From ias_frm_fld_priv
and Tab_name=Upper(P_Tab_Nm)
and Fld_name=Upper(P_Fld_Nm)

P_Ad_Date In Date ) RETURN NUMBER Is
V_Doc_Day_Prd Privilege_Fixed.Upd_Doc_Day_Prd%Type ;
V_Doc_Hrs_Prd Privilege_Fixed.Upd_Doc_Hrs_Prd%Type ;
V_Ret_Value Number := 0 ;
Select Upd_Doc_Day_Prd ,
Upd_Doc_Hrs_Prd
Into V_Doc_Day_Prd ,
V_Doc_Hrs_Prd
From Privilege_Fixed
Where U_Id = P_UserNo ;
If Nvl(V_Doc_Day_Prd,0) <> 0 Then
If P_Ad_Date + V_Doc_Day_Prd < Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Curdate Then
V_Ret_Value := 1 ;
End If ;
If Nvl(V_Doc_HRS_Prd,0) <> 0 Then
If P_Ad_Date + (V_Doc_HRS_Prd/24) < Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Curdate Then
V_Ret_Value := 2 ;
Return(V_Ret_Value);
End Get_Update_Doc_Period ;
V_Doc_Day_Prd Privilege_Fixed.Del_Doc_Day_Prd%Type ;
V_Doc_Hrs_Prd Privilege_Fixed.Del_Doc_Hrs_Prd%Type ;
Select Del_Doc_Day_Prd ,
Del_Doc_Hrs_Prd
End Get_Delete_Doc_Period ;

FUNCTION CHK_FLD_MNDTRY (P_FRM_NO IN FORM_DETAIL.FORM_NO%TYPE ,P_TBL_NM IN VARCHAR2 , P_FLD_NM IN VARCHAR2 ) RETURN NUMBER IS

CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE IAS_Acode_Pkg as
FUNCTION Get_A_Name(P_a_code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE,P_Lng_no In Number) RETURN VARCHAR2;
FUNCTION Get_Ac_code_By_Nm (P_a_nm In ACCOUNT.A_NAME%TYPE) RETURN VARCHAR2 ;
FUNCTION Get_Cnt_Acy (P_a_code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE,P_Usr_No In Number) RETURN NUMBER;
FUNCTION Check_Acy (P_a_code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE,P_a_cy In VARCHAR2) RETURN NUMBER;
FUNCTION Get_Ac_Type (P_a_code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE,P_Type In VARCHAR2 Default Null) RETURN NUMBER;
FUNCTION Get_A_S_M (P_A_S_M In ACCOUNT.A_S_M%TYPE) RETURN Number;
FUNCTION Get_AC_PARENT(P_a_code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE, P_G_PARENT NUMBER DEFAULT 0) RETURN VARCHAR2;
FUNCTION Get_AC_Analysis(P_a_code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE) RETURN NUMBER;
FUNCTION Get_Ac_Dtl_Typ (P_A_Code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE) RETURN NUMBER ;
End IAS_Acode_Pkg;
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY IAS_Acode_Pkg as
--================================================================
FUNCTION Get_A_Name (P_a_code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE,P_Lng_no In Number) RETURN VARCHAR2 Is
v_name varchar2(60);
select Decode(P_Lng_no,1,a_name,nvl(a_name_Eng,a_name))
Into v_name
From account
where a_code=P_a_code ;
RETURN(v_name);
RETURN(Null);
End Get_A_Name ;
FUNCTION Get_Ac_code_By_Nm (P_a_nm In ACCOUNT.A_NAME%TYPE) RETURN VARCHAR2 Is
v_code varchar2(30);
select A_code
Into v_code
From ACCOUNT
where (a_name = P_a_nm or a_name_eng =P_a_nm )
and Rownum<=1;
RETURN(v_code);
End Get_Ac_code_By_Nm;
--================================================================--================================================================
FUNCTION Get_Cnt_Acy (P_a_code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE,P_Usr_no In Number) RETURN Number Is
Select count(*)
Into Cnt
From Account_Curr
Where A_code =P_A_Code ;
Cnt := 0;
RETURN(Cnt);
End Get_Cnt_Acy ;
FUNCTION Get_Ac_Type (P_a_code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE,P_Type In VARCHAR2 Default Null) RETURN Number Is
-- RETURN 1 - Cash 2 - Bank 3 - Inv Acc Code 4 - Cost acc Code
If Nvl(P_Type,'CSH')='CSH' Then
nto cnt
From cash_in_hand
Where a_code=P_a_code
Select 2
Into cnt
From cash_at_bank
Cnt :=0;
RETURN(cnt);
RETURN(0);
End Get_Ac_Type ;
--===============================================================
FUNCTION Check_Acy (P_a_code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE,P_a_cy In VARCHAR2) RETURN Number Is
From account_curr
where a_code=P_a_code
and a_cy =P_a_cy
End Check_Acy ;
--======================================
=========================
FUNCTION Get_A_S_M (P_a_S_M In ACCOUNT.A_S_m%TYPE) RETURN Number Is
Select Decode(Count(Distinct A_LEVEL),1,0,Min(a_Level))
where a_s_m=P_a_s_m;
RETURN(null);
End Get_A_S_M ;
FUNCTION GET_AC_PARENT(P_a_code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE, P_G_PARENT NUMBER DEFAULT 0) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
V_Parnt_code Varchar2(30);
If P_G_PARENT = 1 Then -- Get Garent Parent
Declare
Cursor Ac_cv Is Select a_code From Account
Start with A_code= P_a_code
Connect by A_code = Prior A_PARENT Order by Level;
For i in Ac_cv Loop
V_Parnt_code:=i.A_code ;
End loop;
V_Parnt_code:=NULL;
Else -- Get Parent Account
Select A_Parent
Into V_Parnt_code
From Account
Where A_code=P_a_code ;
RETURN(V_Parnt_code);
RETURN(NULL);
End GET_AC_PARENT;
FUNCTION Get_AC_Analysis(P_a_code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE) RETURN NUMBER Is
v_a_analysis Number;
Select A_ANALYSIS
Into v_a_analysis
RETURN(v_a_analysis);
End Get_AC_Analysis ;
FUNCTION Get_Ac_Dtl_Typ (P_A_Code IN ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE) RETURN NUMBER Is
V_Ac_Dtl_Typ ACCOUNT.AC_DTL_TYP%TYPE;
Select nvl(AC_DTL_TYP,0)
Into V_Ac_Dtl_Typ
where A_CODE=P_A_Code
RETURN(V_Ac_Dtl_Typ);
End Get_Ac_Dtl_Typ ;
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE IAS_Brn_Pkg as
FUNCTION Get_Br_Nm ( G_BRN_NO In S_BRN.BRN_NO%TYPE , P_Lng_no In Number) Return VARCHAR2;
FUNCTION Get_Br_Cmp ( G_BRN_NO In S_BRN.BRN_NO%TYPE ) Return Number;
PROCEDURE Get_Brn_Cmp_Ser ( G_BRN_NO In S_BRN.BRN_NO%TYPE , P_CMP_NO In Out S_BRN.Cmp_NO%TYPE) ;

FUNCTION Chk_Usr_Brn_Prv( P_UserNo In Number , G_BRN_NO In Number,P_Priv_Type In Varchar2) RETURN NUMBER Is

Into v_Flg

Return(v_Flg);
End Chk_Usr_Brn_Prv;

And Cmp_No = P_CMP_NO
Cnt:=0;

End Chk_Brn_Cmp_Ser ;

Execute Immediate
'Select '||P_Inf_Type||'
where Brn_No = '||G_BRN_NO||' and RowNum<=1 '
Into v_Inf ;
v_Inf:=Null;
V_Inf:=Null;
Return(v_Inf);
End Get_Br_Inf ;

Procedure Set_Sys_Prmtrs ( Lang_no NUMBER ,U_id NUMBER) Is
User_Id :=U_id;
Lng_no :=Lang_no;
End Set_Sys_Prmtrs;
--===========================================================================================================
V_Min_Rate Number;
V_Max_Rate Number;
f P_Accur Is Not Null And P_Accur <> P_Local_Cur Then
Select Nvl(Min_Cur_Rate,0) ,Nvl(Max_Cur_Rate,0)
Into V_Min_Rate,V_Max_Rate
Where Cur_Code = P_Accur ;
If P_Acrate Is Not Null Then
If Nvl(P_Acrate,0) > Nvl(V_Max_Rate,0) And Nvl(V_Max_Rate,0) > 0 Then
Return(2); --Message No= 593
Elsif Nvl(P_Acrate,0) < Nvl(V_Min_Rate,0) And Nvl(V_Min_Rate,0) > 0 Then
Return(3); --Message No= 594
Return(1);
Raise_Application_Error(-20408,' Error When Get Limit CurrencyRate = '||Sqlerrm) ;
End Check_Cur_Rate;
PROCEDURE Check_Date( P_Doc_Date In Date,
P_Scr_Type In Varchar2,
P_Lang_No In Number Default 1,
P_Brn_No In Number Default NULL) IS
v_s Number;
v_fs Number;
sd Date;
--P_Scr_Type FRM Or REP
--P_Frm_Type 0 = Not Inv Scr, 1= Inv Scr

If Upper(P_Scr_Type) = 'FRM' Then --(2)
IF P_Brn_No is null then
Select nvl(INACTV,0), 0
Into v_s ,v_fs
where P_doc_date between F_date and t_date;
Select nvl(INACTV,0),nvl(PRD_CLS,0)
From S_PRD_BRN_CLS
where P_doc_date between F_date and t_date
AND BRN_NO = P_BRN_NO;
If v_s=1 and Upper(P_Scr_Type) = 'FRM' Then
Raise_application_error(-20605,ias_gen_pkg.get_msg(P_Lang_no,1096));
ElsIf v_fs=1 and Upper(P_Scr_Type) = 'FRM' and P_chk_cls = 1 Then
/*If P_chk_cls = 1 Then
Select Status Into cnt From IAS_CLOSE_DAY_PERIODS
where P_doc_date between f_date and t_date
If cnt>0 and Upper(P_Scr_Type) = 'FRM' Then
--If v_fld Is Not Null Then
-- Copy(Null,v_fld);
--End if;
when no_data_found then
End If;*/
when no_data_found th
Raise_application_error(-20605,ias_gen_pkg.get_msg(P_Lang_no,9));
--## Date Lock
If Upper(P_Scr_Type) = 'FRM' Then --(3)
/* Select Count(1) Into cnt
From date_lock
where P_doc_date between ufd and utd or P_doc_date between lfd and ltd;
If cnt>0 Then
Select Count(1) Into cnt
From ias_date_lock_User
where u_id = User_Id
and (P_doc_date between ufd and utd or P_doc_date between lfd and ltd);
If cnt>0 then
--## check inv close.
If P_Frm_Type = 1 Then
where INV_CLS = 1;

--## sysdate greater then today
/* Select Sysdate Into sd From dual;
If P_doc_date> sd then
MsgBox(365);
End Check_Date;
PROCEDURE Check_Bt_Date (P_Fd In Out Date,P_Td In Out Date) Is
TempDate Date;
If P_Fd Is Null and P_Td Is Not Null Then
P_Fd:=P_Td;
--ElsIf P_Fd Is Not Null and P_Td Is Null Then
--P_Td:=P_Fd;
If P_Fd > P_Td Then
TempDate:=P_Fd;
P_Td:=TempDate;
End if;
End Check_Bt_Date;
PROCEDURE Check_Bt_Value (P_FN In Out Varchar2,P_TN In Out Varchar2,P_Type In Varchar2 Default 'N' ) Is
TempNm Number;
TempCh Varchar2(30);
If P_FN Is Null and P_TN Is Not Null Then
P_FN:=P_TN;
--ElsIf P_FN Is Not Null and P_TN Is Null Then
-- P_TN:=P_FN;
If Upper(P_type) = 'N' Then -- Numeric
If To_number(P_FN) > To_number(P_TN) Then
TempNm:=To_number(P_FN);
P_TN:=TempNm;
Else -- Alph
If P_FN > P_TN Then
TempCh:=P_FN;
P_TN:=TempCh;
End Check_Bt_Value;
--============================================================================
(P_Type In varchar2,P_a_code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE,P_acy In varchar2) RETURN NUMBER Is
v_Inc Number:=0;
If P_a_code Is Not Null and P_acy Is Not Null Then
If Upper(P_type) ='ACODE' Then
Select nvl(Inactive,0)
Into v_Inc
From Account_curr
Where a_code = P_a_code
and a_cy=P_acy;
Elsif Upper(P_type) ='CCODE' Then
From customer_curr
Where c_code = P_a_code
Elsif Upper(P_type) ='CASH' Then
From Ias_cash_in_hand_dtl
Where cash_no = P_a_code
Elsif Upper(P_type) ='BANK' Then
From Ias_cash_at_bank_dtl
Where bank_no = P_a_code
Return(v_Inc);
End Check_Ac_Acy_Inctv;
--=================================================================
(P_a_code In ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE) RETURN NUMBER Is
v_Use_cc Number;
Select nvl(Use_cc,1)
Into v_Use_cc
Return(v_Use_cc);
End Check_Use_Cc;
FUNCTION Check_Inv_Close RETURN NUMBER Is
Select 1 Into cnt From S_PRD_MST
Where inv_cls = 1
Return(cnt);
when o
thers then
End Check_Inv_Close;
Function Check_Lmt_Cost_Itm ( P_Wtavg_Type In Number,
P_I_Code In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_Code%Type,
P_W_Code In Warehouse_Details.W_Code%Type Default Null,
P_Itm_Cost In Number) Return Boolean Is
V_Cnt Number;
If P_Wtavg_Type = 1 Then -- By Items
From Ias_Itm_Mst
Where I_Code =P_I_Code
And (Nvl(P_Itm_Cost,0) >= Nvl(I_Cwtavg,0) - (Nvl(I_Cwtavg,0) * Nvl(Min_Lmt_Cost_Per,0))/100 Or Min_Lmt_Cost_Per Is Null)
And (Nvl(P_Itm_Cost,0) <= Nvl(I_Cwtavg,0) + (Nvl(I_Cwtavg,0) * Nvl(Max_Lmt_Cost_Per,0))/100 Or Max_Lmt_Cost_Per Is Null Or Nvl(I_Cwtavg,0) = 0)
And Rownum<=1;
Return TRUE;
Elsif P_Wtavg_Type In (2,3) And P_W_Code Is Not Null Then -- By Items + W_code
Into V_Cnt
From Ias_Itm_Wcode
And W_Code = P_W_Code
V_Cnt:=0;
If Nvl(V_Cnt,0)>0 Then
/*Select 1
Into V_cnt
Where i_code =P_I_Code
and w_code = P_W_code
And (Nvl(P_Itm_Cost,0) >= Nvl(I_cwtavg,0) - (Nvl(I_cwtavg,0) * Nvl(MIN_LMT_COST_PER,0))/100 Or MIN_LMT_COST_PER Is Null)
And (Nvl(P_Itm_Cost,0) <= Nvl(I_cwtavg,0) + (Nvl(I_cwtavg,0) * Nvl(MAX_LMT_COST_PER,0))/100 Or MAX_LMT_COST_PER Is Null Or Nvl(I_cwtavg,0) = 0)
And RowNum<=1;*/
From Ias_Itm_Wcode ,Ias_Itm_Mst
Where Ias_Itm_Mst.I_Code=Ias_Itm_Wcode.I_Code
And Ias_Itm_Mst.I_Code =P_I_Code
And (Nvl(P_Itm_Cost,0) >= Nvl(Ias_Itm_Wcode.I_Cwtavg,0) - (Nvl(Ias_Itm_Wcode.I_Cwtavg,0) * Nvl(Ias_Itm_Mst.Min_Lmt_Cost_Per,0))/100 Or Ias_Itm_Mst.Min_Lmt_Cost_Per Is Null)
And (Nvl(P_Itm_Cost,0) <= Nvl(Ias_Itm_Wcode.I_Cwtavg,0) + (Nvl(Ias_Itm_Wcode.I_Cwtavg,0) * Nvl(Ias_Itm_Mst.Max_Lmt_Cost_Per,0))/100 Or Ias_Itm_Mst.Max_Lmt_Cost_Per Is Null Or Nvl(Ias_Itm_Wcode.I_Cwtavg,0) = 0)
Return FALSE;
End Check_Lmt_Cost_Itm ;
--================================== End Pkg ====================================
End IAS_Check_Sys_Pkg ;

FUNCTION Check_Cons_St ( P_ConsNm In VARCHAR2 Default Null,
FUNCTION Get_Cons_Name ( P_TabNm In VARCHAR2 Default Null,P_ConsType In VARCHAR2 Default Null ,
P_ColNm In VARCHAR2 Default Null) RETURN CHAR ;
FUNCTION Check_Object ( P_ObjNm In VARCHAR2 ,P_ObjType In VARCHAR2 Default Null , P_ColNm In VARCHAR2 Default Null
) RETURN NUMBER ;
PROCEDURE Check_St_OBJ ( P_Result In Out NUMBER ,P_ObjNm In VARCHAR2 Default Null) ;
FUNCTION Check_TabSp ( P_TsNm In VARCHAR2 ) RETURN NUMBER ;
FUNCTION Check_Ias_User( P_Brn_Year In number ,P_Brn_no In number) RETURN varchar2 ;

P_TabNm In varchar2,
P_ColNm In varchar2 Default Null,
P_Usr_Nm In varchar2 Default Null) RETURN Varchar2 ;

End IAS_DBS_Sys_Pkg;
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE Body IAS_DBS_Sys_Pkg IS
--==========================================================================================
FUNCTION Check_Cons ( P_ConsNm In VARCHAR2 Default Null,
P_ColNm In VARCHAR2 Default Null) RETURN NUMBER Is
Cnt number;
Select Count(1)
From User_constraints a, User_cons_columns b
Where a.owner=b.owner
and a.constraint_name=b.constraint_name
and a.table_name=b.table_name
and a.table_
name=Decode(P_TabNm,null,a.table_name,Upper(P_TabNm))
and b.column_name=Decode(P_ColNm,null,b.column_name,Upper(P_ColNm))
and a.constraint_type=Decode(P_ConsType,null,a.constraint_type,Upper(P_ConsType))
and a.constraint_name=Decode(P_ConsNm,null,a.constraint_name,Upper(P_ConsNm));
End Check_Cons;
and a.table_name=Decode(P_TabNm,null,a.table_name,Upper(P_TabNm))
and a.constraint_name=Decode(P_ConsNm,null,a.constraint_name,Upper(P_ConsNm))
and a.status = 'DISABLED';
End Check_Cons_St;
P_ColNm In VARCHAR2 Default Null) RETURN CHAR Is
v_ConsNm Varchar2(50);
Select a.constraint_name Into v_ConsNm
From User_constraints a,User_cons_columns b
where a.owner=b.owner
RETURN(v_ConsNm);
RETURN Null;
End Get_Cons_Name;
FUNCTION Check_Object ( P_ObjNm In VARCHAR2 , P_ObjType In VARCHAR2 Default Null , P_ColNm In VARCHAR2 Default Null) RETURN NUMBER IS
Cnt NUMBER;
If P_ObjNm Is Not Null and P_ColNm Is Not Null Then
Select 1 Into Cnt
From User_Tab_Columns
where Table_Name =Upper(P_ObjNm)
and Column_name=Upper(P_ColNm)
From User_Objects
Where Object_Name = Upper(P_ObjNm)
and OBJECT_TYPE = Decode(p_ObjType, Null,OBJECT_TYPE,Upper(p_ObjType))
END Check_Obj
ect;
PROCEDURE Check_St_OBJ ( P_Result In Out NUMBER ,P_ObjNm In VARCHAR2 Default Null ) Is
Into P_Result
where STATUS='INVALID'
and OBJECT_NAME =Decode( P_ObjNm,null,OBJECT_NAME,Upper(P_ObjNm))
If p_Result > 0 Then
Cursor c1 Is Select Decode(OBJECT_TYPE,'PACKAGE BODY','PACKAGE',OBJECT_TYPE) OBJECT_TYPE,OBJECT_NAME
From User_objects
Where Status='INVALID'
and OBJECT_TYPE<>'TABLE';
For i in c1 Loop
Execute Immediate 'ALTER '||i.OBJECT_TYPE||' '||i.OBJECT_NAME||' COMPILE';
p_Result:=0;
End Check_St_OBJ;
FUNCTION Check_TabSp ( P_TsNm In VARCHAR2 ) RETURN NUMBER Is
From User_Tablespaces
where TABLESPACE_NAME =Upper(P_TsNm)
END Check_TabSp;
Function Check_Ias_User( P_Brn_Year In Number
,P_Brn_no In Number)
RETURN varchar2 Is
V_User Varchar2(30);
Select UserName
Into V_User
From All_Users
where USERNAME='IAS'||P_Brn_Year||P_Brn_No
and rownum <= 1;
--when no_data_found then
--null;
Raise_Application_Error(-20460,'Error when checking Accounting user existance' || chr(10) || sqlerrm);
RETURN Upper(V_User);
End Check_Ias_User;
--==============================================================================
FUNCTION Get_Comment ( P_Lng In Number,
P_TabNm In Varchar2,
P_ColNm In Varchar2 Default Null,
P_Usr_Nm In Varchar2 Default Null) RETURN varchar2 Is
v_com_dsc Varchar2(800);
v_com_no Number;
If P_TabNm Is Not Null and P_ColNm Is Null Then -- Table
If P_Usr_Nm Is Null Then
Select COMMENTS
Into v_com_dsc
From User_Tab_Comments
where TABLE_NAME = P_TabNm
From ALL_Tab_Comments
where OWNER = P_Usr_Nm
and TABLE_NAME = P_TabNm
ElsIf P_TabNm Is Not Null and P_ColNm Is Not Null Then -- Columns
From User_Col_Comments
and COLUMN_NAME =P_ColNm
From ALL_Col_Comments
where Owner = P_Usr_Nm
v_com_no:=v_com_dsc;
v_com_no:=null;
If v_com_no Is Not Null Then
RETURN(Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(P_Lng,v_com_no));
Elsif v_com_dsc Is Not Null Then
RETURN(v_com_dsc);
End Get_Comment;
PROCEDURE ALTR_DBS( P_TB IN VARCHAR2, P_TR_TYPE IN VARCHAR2,P_FD IN VARCHAR2) Is
Execute Immediate 'ALTER TABLE '||P_TB||' '||P_TR_TYPE||' '||P_FD;
End ALTR_DBS;
--=======================================================================================
End IAS_DBS_Sys_Pkg

-----------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Get_Itm_Name (P_Lng_no In Number,P_icode In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_CODE%TYPE,P_i_name Out varchar2,P_idesc Out varchar2) IS
Select Decode(P_lng_no,1,nvl(I_name,I_e_name),nvl(I_e_name,I_name)),i_desc
Into P_i_name,P_idesc
Where i_code=P_icode;
--Raise_Application_Error(-20410,'Item Code Is Invalid = '||SqlErrm) ;
Raise_application_error(-20410,Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(P_Lng_No,46)||' ,
'||SqlErrm);
End Get_Itm_Name;
------------------------------------------------------------
P_Blocked Out Number) IS
Select Decode(P_lng_no,1,nvl(I_name,I_e_name),nvl(I_e_name,I_name)),i_desc,
nvl(Use_Exp_date,0),nvl(Use_batch_no,0),nvl(Use_Serialno,0),
nvl(Use_Qty_Fraction,0),nvl(Allow_Free_Qty,0),nvl(Allow_Disc,0),
nvl(Service_Itm,0),nvl(Kit_Itm,0),nvl(Blocked,0)
Into P_i_name,P_idesc,P_UseExpDt ,
P_UseBtch, P_UseSer, P_UseFrc,P_UseFree,P_UseDisc,P_Srv_Itm ,
P_Kit_Itm,P_Blocked
--Raise_Application_Error(-20411,'Item Code Is Invalid = '||SqlErrm) ;
Raise_application_error(-20411,Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(P_Lng_No,46)||' ,'||
SqlErrm);
End Get_Itm_Info ;
FUNCTION Get_Grand_Avlqty( P_Icode In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_CODE%TYPE,
P_Wcode In WAREHOUSE_DETAILS.W_CODE%TYPE Default null) Return NUMBER Is
avlq NUMBER;
If P_wcode Is Null Then
Select Sum(avlq)
Into avlq
From ( Select sum(distinct Avl_qty) avlq,w_code
Where Ias_Itm_Wcode.I_Code = P_Icode
And Ias_Itm_Wcode.P_Size = 1
group by w_code );
Select Sum(distinct Avl_qty)
Into avlq From Ias_Itm_Wcode
where Ias_Itm_Wcode.I_Code = P_Icode
And W_code=P_wcode;
Return(Nvl(avlq,0));
End Get_Grand_Avlqty;
FUNCTION Get_Grand_Wtavg( P_Wtavg_Type In NUMBER,
P_Wcode In WAREHOUSE_DETAILS.W_CODE%TYPE Default Null ) Return NUMBER Is
v_Wtavg NUMBER;
If P_Icode Is Not Null Then
If P_Wtavg_Type = 1 Then --By Item
Select nvl(i_cwtavg,0)
Into v_Wtavg
Where i_code = P_Icode;
ElsIf P_Wtavg_Type = 2 Then --By Item + W_Code
Where i_code = P_Icode
And w_code = P_Wcode
And P_Size = 1 ;
ElsIf P_Wtavg_Type = 3 Then -- By Item + Whg_Code
Select Nvl(I_Cwtavg,0)
Into V_Wtavg
Where I_Code = P_Icode
And Whg_Code = Ias_Wcode_Pkg.Get_Whg_Code (P_Wcode)
And P_Size = 1
And Rownum <= 1 ;
V_Wtavg := 0 ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20411,'Error In Get Wtavg For I_Code '||P_Icode||Sqlerrm);
--Raise_application_error(-20412,Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(P_Lng_No,46)||' ,'||SqlErrm);
Raise_Application_Error(-20412,'Error In get wtavg for i_code '||P_Icode||SqlErrm);
return(v_Wtavg);
End Get_Grand_Wtavg;

Cnt Number;
where i_code = P_icode

when No_Data_Found Then

FUNCTION Check_Kit_Itm(P_icode In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_CODE%TYPE) Return NUMBER Is
and Kit_Itm = 1
Raise_ApplICation_Error(-20414,'Error In Check_Kit_Itm ' || '' || SqlErrm);
End Check_Kit_Itm;
P_Itm_Unt In Ias_Itm_Dtl.Itm_Unt%TYPE) Return NUMBER Is
From Ias_Itm_Dtl
where I_Code = P_Icode
And Itm_Unt = P_Itm_Unt
And No_Sale = 1
And RowNum <=1;
Raise_ApplICation_Error(-20415,'Error In Check_NoSale_Itm ' || '' || SqlErrm);

FUNCTION Check_Itm_Wcode_Inactv (P_I_code In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_CODE%TYPE,P_w_code In Warehouse_details.W_CODE%TYPE) RETURN NUMBER Is
v_Inactv Number;

From Ias_Itm_wcode
Where i_code = P_i_code
and w_code = P_w_code
Return(v_Inactv);
End Check_Itm_Wcode_Inactv;
PROCEDURE Get_I_Code( P_Barcode In IAS_ITM_UNT_BARCODE.BARCODE%TYPE ,P_I_Code IN OUT Ias_Itm_Mst.I_Code%Type ,P_Itm_Unt IN OUT Ias_Itm_Dtl.Itm_Unt%Type) Is
v_icode varchar2(30);
select I_Code ,Itm_Unt
Into P_I_Code,P_Itm_Unt
From Ias_Itm_Unt_Barcode
where Barcode=P_Barcode;
Select I_Code, Itm_Unt
Into P_I_Code, P_Itm_Unt
Where Barcode = P_Barcode
and Barcode<>i_code;
End Get_I_Code ;
Function Fetch_Itm_Nm_Price_Order (P_I_Code In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_Code%Type )Return Varchar2 Is
V_I_Nm Ias_Itm_Mst.I_Name%Type ;
Select Decode(1,1,Nvl(I_Name,I_E_Name),Nvl(I_E_Name,I_Name)) I_Name
Into V_I_Nm
Where I_Code = P_I_Code ;
Return(V_I_Nm);
FUNCTION Get_MaxGrand_Size ( P_icode In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_CODE%TYPE) return NUMBER is
v_gsize NUMBER;
select max(P_Size) into v_gsize From Ias_Itm_Dtl where I_Code= P_Icode ;
return(v_gsize);
exception when others then
return(1);
End Get_MaxGrand_Size ;
Function Get_Itm_Activity (P_I_Code In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_Code%Type ) Return Number Is
V_Actv_No Ias_Items_Activity.Activity_No%Type ;
Select Activity_No
Into V_Actv_No
Return(V_Actv_No) ;
P_Actv_No Out Ias_Actvty.Actv_No%Type ) Is
Select Cc_Code ,
Pj_No ,
Actv_No
Into P_Cc_Code ,
P_Pj_No ,
P_Actv_No
From Ias_Conn_Wcode_By_Activity
Where W_Code = P_W_Code
And Activity_No = P_Itm_Actv_No ;
P_Cc_Code := Null ;
P_Pj_No := Null ;
P_Actv_No := Null ;
End Get_Itm_Activity_Wcode_Info ;
Function Get_Icode_One_Unit (P_I_Code In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_Code%Type) Return Ias_Itm_Dtl.Itm_Unt%Type Is
V_Itm_Unt Ias_Itm_Dtl.Itm_Unt%Type;
Select Itm_Unt Into V_Itm_Unt
Where Ias_Itm_Dtl.I_Code = P_I_Code;
Return(V_Itm_Unt);
End Get_Icode_One_Unit ;
Function Get_Icode_Unit_By_Lvl (P_I_Code In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_Code%Type , P_Lvl_Unit In Ias_Itm_Dtl.Lvl_Unit%Type) Return Ias_Itm_Dtl.Itm_Unt%Type Is
Where Ias_Itm_Dtl.I_Code = P_I_Code
And Lvl_Unit=P_Lvl_Unit
And RownuM<=1;
End Get_Icode_Unit_By_Lvl ;
Function Chk_Icode_Unit ( P_I_Code In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_Code%Type ,
P_Itm_Unt In Ias_Itm_Dtl.Itm_Unt%Type ) Return Number Is
V_Cnt Number := 1 ;
V_Inactv Number := 0 ;
Select Inactive Into V_Inactv
And Ias_Itm_Dtl.Itm_Unt = P_Itm_Unt
And RowNum <= 1 ;
/*When No_Data_Found Then
V_Cnt := 0 ;
When Too_Many_Rows Then
V_Cnt := 1 ;*/
If Nvl(V_Cnt,0) = 0 Then
Return (44);
ElsIf Nvl(V_Inactv,0) = 1 Then
Return (377);
End Chk_Icode_Unit ;
Function Get_Icode_Min_Unit (P_I_Code In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_Code%Type ) Return Ias_Itm_Dtl.Itm_Unt%Type Is
V_Itm_Unt Ias_Itm_Dtl.Itm_Unt%Type ;
And Nvl(Ias_Itm_Dtl.P_Size,0) = 1
End Get_Icode_Min_Unit ;
Function Get_Icode_Sales_Unit (P_I_Code In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_Code%Type ) Return Ias_Itm_Dtl.Itm_Unt%Type Is
--------------------------------------------------------
And Nvl(Ias_Itm_Dtl.No_Sale,0)=0;
V_Itm_Unt := Null;
If V_Itm_Unt Is Null Then
And Nvl(Ias_Itm_Dtl.Sale_Unit,0) = 1
And Nvl(Ias_Itm_Dtl.No_Sale,0) = 0
End Get_Icode_Sales_Unit ;
--##----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
------------##--
Function Get_Icode_Purch_Unit (P_I_Code In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_Code%Type ) Return Ias_Itm_Dtl.Itm_Unt%Type Is
Where Ias_Itm_Dtl.I_Code = P_I_Code ;
And Nvl(Ias_Itm_Dtl.Pur_Unit,0) = 1
End Get_Icode_Purch_Unit ;
Function Get_Icode_Size_Unit (P_I_Code In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_Code%Type ,P_Itm_Unt In Ias_Itm_Dtl.Itm_Unt%Type) Return Number Is
V_Psize Number ;
Select P_Size Into V_Psize
Where I_Code = P_I_Code
And Itm_Unt=P_Itm_Unt
Return(V_Psize);
Return (1);
End Get_Icode_Size_Unit ;

Create Or Replace Package Body Ias_Pos_Distibuted_Db_Pkg As
Function Get_Date (Sqlstr Varchar2) Return Date Is
Val Date;
Execute Immediate Sqlstr Into Val;
Return Val;
Return Null ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20001,'Error Get_Date ' || '' || Sqlerrm);
End Get_Date ;
V_Val2 Out Number ) Is
Execute Immediate Sqlstr Into V_Chr,V_Val1,V_Val2 ;
Null ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20002,'Error Get_Chr_Tow_Value ' || '' || Sqlerrm);
End Get_Chr_Tow_Value ;
P_Db_Link In Varchar2 ) Is
V_Use_Prev_Paid_Card Number;
V_Use_Card_More_One Number;
begin
Select Use_Prev_Paid_Card,Use_Card_More_One
Into V_Use_Prev_Paid_Card,V_Use_Card_More_One
From Ias_Para_Pos;
V_Use_Prev_Paid_Card:=0;
V_Use_Card_More_One :=0;
If Nvl(V_Use_Prev_Paid_Card,0)=1 Then
If P_Card_No is null Then
Execute Immediate ' DELETE FROM IAS_POS_CUST_CARD_AMT_TMP ';
Execute Immediate ' insert into Ias_Pos_Cust_Card_Amt_tmp
select card_no from
(select card_no,REM_CARD_AMT,PROCESSED From Ias_Pos_Customer_Card_Amount Where Nvl(Processed,0)=0
minus
select card_no,REM_CARD_AMT,PROCESSED From '||User||'.Ias_Pos_Customer_Card_Amount@'||P_Db_Link||')';
If Nvl(V_Use_Card_More_One,0) =1 Then
Cursor C_Card Is Select Card_No,Rem_Card_Amt
From Ias_Pos_Customer_Card_Amount
Where Exists(Select 1
From Ias_Pos_Cust_Card_Amt_tmp
Where Ias_Pos_Cust_Card_Amt_tmp.Card_No=Ias_Pos_Customer_Card_Amount.Card_No
And RowNum <= 1 )
Order By Card_No;
V_Rem_Amt_Srv Number;
For I In C_Card Loop
V_Rem_Amt_Srv :=IAS_Gen_Pkg.Get_Cnt ('select REM_CARD_AMT from
'||User||'.Ias_Pos_Customer_Card_Amount@'||P_Db_Link||' Where Card_No = '||I.Card_No);
V_Rem_Amt_Srv:=0 ;
If Nvl(I.Rem_Card_Amt,0)< Nvl(V_Rem_Amt_Srv,0) Then
Execute Immediate ' Update '||User||'.Ias_Pos_Customer_Card_Amount@'||P_Db_Link||'
Set Processed
= 1 ,
Rem_Card_Amt = '||Nvl(I.Rem_Card_Amt,0)||'
Where Card_No = '||I.Card_No ;
End Loop ;
Execute Immediate ' Update '||User||'.Ias_Pos_Customer_Card_Amount@'||P_Db_Link||' a
Set Processed = 1 ,
Rem_Card_Amt = 0
Where Ias_Pos_Cust_Card_Amt_tmp.Card_No=a.Card_No
And RowNum <= 1 )';
Where CARD_NO= '||P_Card_No||'';
Exception when others Then
Raise_Application_Error(-20002,'Error updatee ' || '' || Sqlerrm);
End Update_Card_In_Db_Link ;
P_Usr_No In Number ) Is
Execute Immediate ' Update '||User||'.'||P_Table_Nm||'@'||P_Db_Link||'
Set Payed = 1 ,
-- Bill_No = '||P_Bill_No ||' ,
Machine_No_Paid = '||P_Machine_No||' ,
Paid_U_Id = '||P_Usr_No ||' ,
Paid_Date = SysDate
Where Rt_Bill_No = '||P_Rt_Bill_No ;
P_Bill_No In Out Number ) Is
Execute Immediate P_Sqlstr Into P_Rt_Bill_Date ,
P_Return_Type ,
P_A_Cy ,
P_Rt_Bill_Rate ,
P_Rt_Bill_Amt ,
P_Disc_Amt ,
P_Vat_Amt ,
P_Cheque_Amt ,
P_Machine_No ,
P_Ad_U_Id ,
P_Ad_Date ,
P_Machine_No_Rt_Bill,
P_Bill_No ;
End Get_PayCash_RtBillNo_Info ;
P_Machine_No In Out Number ) Is
Execute Immediate P_Sqlstr Into P_Bill_Date ,
P_Machine_No
End Get_PayCash_BillNo_Info ;
P_Vat_Amt In Out Number ) Is
Execute Immediate P_Sqlstr Into P_P_Qty ,
P_I_Qty ,
P_I_Price ,
P_dis_amt_mst ,
P_dis_amt_dtl ,
P_Vat_Amt ;
End Get_Price_From_Bill ;

CREATE OR REPLACE Package Body Ias_Pos_Pkg Is
--===============================insert_pos_bills=======================================
Procedure insert_pos_bills(p_yr_br In Varchar2,P_Db_Link In Varchar2) Is
Execute Immediate 'Insert Into '||p_yr_br||'.ias_pos_bill_mst@'||P_Db_Link||'(
Bill_No, Bill_Date, Bill_Time,Bill_Type, C_Code, A_Cy,
Bill_Rate, Bill_Amt, Posted,Bill_Note, Machine_No, Hung,
Payed_Amt, Reported, Vat_Amt,Disc_Amt, Disc_Amt_Mst, Disc_Amt_Dtl,
Cheque_No, Cheque_Amt, Cheque_Due_Date,Credit_Card, Cr_Card_No, Cr_Card_Amt,
Cr_Card_No_Scnd, Cr_Card_Amt_Scnd, Cr_Card_No_Thrd,Cr_Card_Amt_Thrd, Cash_No,
W_Code,Hung_U_Id, Hung_Date, Paid_U_Id,Paid_Date, Rep_Code, Emp_No,
Ret_Amt,Card_Amt,Bill_Valued, Cr_Doc_No_Ref, Value_Date,Paid_Bill,Cr_Valued_Thrd,
Cr_Valued_Scnd,Cr_Valued, Cr_Doc_No_Ref_Scnd, Cr_Doc_No_Ref_Thrd,
Cr_Value_Date, Cr_Value_Date_Scnd,Cr_Value_Date_Thrd ,Field1,
Field2, Field3, Field4, Field5,Ad_U_Id, Ad_Date, Up_U_Id,
Up_Date, Cmp_No, Brn_No,Brn_Year, Brn_Usr,
SI_TYPE,Cr_Card_Comm_Per, Cr_Card_Comm_Per_Scnd,Cr_Card_Comm_Per_Thrd,
Qt_Prm_No,Qt_Prm_Ser,Qt_Prm_Rcrd_No,
Mobile_No, Cust_Code, Point_Rplc_Amt, Point_Typ_No, Point_Calc_Typ_No,
Up_Trmnl_Nm, Ad_Trmnl_Nm, Cr_Card_Dsc, Cr_Card_Dsc_Scnd, Cr_Card_Dsc_Thrd,CRD_NO,CRD_DISC_PER)
Select Bill_No, Bill_Date, Bill_Time,Bill_Type, C_Code, A_Cy,
Mobile_No, Cust_Code,Point_Rplc_Amt, Point_Typ_No, Point_Calc_Typ_No,
Up_Trmnl_Nm, Ad_Trmnl_Nm, Cr_Card_Dsc, Cr_Card_Dsc_Scnd, Cr_Card_Dsc_Thrd,CRD_NO,CRD_DISC_PER
From ias_pos_bill_mst
Where Nvl(posted,0) = 0
And Nvl(hung,0) = 0
And Exists (Select 1 From ias_pos_bill_temp
Where ias_pos_bill_temp.bill_no = ias_pos_bill_mst.bill_no
And RowNum <= 1)';
Execute Immediate 'Insert Into '||p_yr_br||'.ias_pos_pay_bills@'||P_Db_Link||'(
bill_no,pay_cur,pay_amt,pay_rate,bill_pay_amt,Bill_Date, Machine_No,Rt_Bill_No ,Card_No,Bill_Type,C_Code,Ad_Date,Ad_U_Id,brn_no,brn_year,CMP_NO,BRN_USR)
Select bill_no,pay_cur,pay_amt,pay_rate,bill_pay_amt,Bill_Date, Machine_No,Rt_Bill_No ,Card_No,Bill_Type,C_Code,Ad_Date,Ad_U_Id,brn_no,brn_year,CMP_NO,BRN_USR
From ias_pos_pay_bills
Where Exists ( Select 1 From ias_pos_bill_mst
Where ias_pos_pay_bills.bill_no = ias_pos_bill_mst.bill_no
And Nvl(posted,0) =0
Where ias_pos_bill_temp.bill_no = ias_pos_pay_bills.bill_no
And RowNum <= 1 ) ';
Execute Immediate 'Insert Into '||p_yr_br||'.Pos_Point_Calc_trns@'||P_Db_Link||'(
Trns_No, Trns_Type, Trns_Date, Cust_Code, Mobile_No, Point_Typ_No, Bill_No, Rt_Bill_No, Doc_Amt,
A_Cy, Point_Cnt,Machine_No, Ad_U_Id, Ad_Date, Cmp_No, Brn_No, Brn_Year, Brn_Usr)
Select Trns_No, Trns_Type, Trns_Date, Cust_Code, Mobile_No, Point_Typ_No, Bill_No, Rt_Bill_No, Doc_Amt,
A_Cy, Point_Cnt,Machine_No, Ad_U_Id, Ad_Date, Cmp_No, Brn_No, Brn_Year, Brn_Usr
From Pos_Point_Calc_trns
Where Pos_Point_Calc_trns.bill_no = ias_pos_bill_mst.bill_no
And Nvl(ias_pos_bill_mst.posted,0) =0
And Nvl(ias_pos_bill_mst.hung,0) = 0
And Trns_Type In(1,3)
Where ias_pos_bill_temp.bill_no = Pos_Point_Calc_trns.bill_no
Execute Immediate 'Insert Into '||p_yr_br||'.ias_pos_bill_dtl@'||P_Db_Link||'(
Bill_No, I_Code, I_Qty,Free_Qty,I_Price, Dis_Per, Dis_Amt,
Dis_Amt_Mst, Dis_Amt_Dtl, Itm_Unt, P_Size, P_Qty, Vat_Per,
Vat_Amt, Batch_No, Expire_Date,Barcode, W_Code, Service_Item,
Cmp_No, Brn_No, Brn_Year,Brn_Usr,
Qt_Prm_No, Qt_Prm_Ser, Qt_Prm_Rcrd_No,RCRD_NO )
Select Bill_No, I_Code, I_Qty,Free_Qty,I_Price, Dis_Per, Dis_Amt,
Qt_Prm_No, Qt_Prm_Ser, Qt_Prm_Rcrd_No,RCRD_NO
From ias_pos_bill_dtl
Where ias_pos_bill_dtl.bill_no = ias_pos_bill_mst.bill_no
Where ias_pos_bill_temp.bill_no = ias_pos_bill_dtl.bill_no
Execute Immediate 'Update ias_pos_bill_mst
Set posted = 1
And RowNum <= 1) ';
Raise_Application_Error(-20001,'Error When Inserting ias_pos_bills '||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
End insert_pos_bills;

os_rt_bill_dtl@'||P_Db_Link||'(

Where ias_pos_rt_bill_temp.rt_bill_no = ias_pos_rt_bill_dtl.rt_bill_no

Procedure Move_Cashier_Deposit(P_Yr_Br In Varchar2,P_Db_Link In Varchar2) Is
Cursor C_Cashier_Deposit_Mst Is Select Doc_No, Doc_Ser,Cashier_No,
Doc_Date, Doc_Desc, Machine_No,External_Post,
Total_Change_Amt,Total_Card_Amt,Total_Paid_Amt,Total_Othr_Amt,
Ad_U_Id, Ad_Date,Up_U_Id, Up_Date,
Cmp_No,Brn_No, Brn_Year, Brn_Usr
From Ias_Deposit_Currency_Mst
Where Nvl(External_Post,0)=0 and Exists(Select 1 From Ias_Deposit_Currency_Tmp
Where Ias_Deposit_Currency_Tmp.Doc_Ser = Ias_Deposit_Currency_Mst.Doc_Ser)
Order By Ad_U_Id,Machine_No,Doc_No ;
V_Cnt Number := 0 ;
V_Doc_No Ias_Deposit_Currency_Mst.Doc_No%Type ;
V_Doc_Ser Ias_Deposit_Currency_Mst.Doc_Ser%Type ;
For I In C_Cashier_Deposit_Mst Loop
--# Get Serial
Execute Immediate ' Select 1 From '||p_yr_br||'.Ias_Deposit_Currency_Mst@'||P_Db_L
ink||'
Where Doc_Ser ='|| I.Doc_Ser
Into V_Cnt ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20001,'Error When Read Cashier Deposit Data From Headoffice'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
If V_Cnt > 0 Then
Execute Immediate ' Select Nvl(Max(Doc_No),0) + 1
From '||p_yr_br||'.Ias_Deposit_Currency_Mst@'||P_Db_Link||'
Where Brn_No = '||I.Brn_No ||'
And Ad_U_Id = '||I.Ad_U_Id ||'
And Machine_No = '||I.Machine_No
Into V_Doc_No ;
V_Doc_Ser := 1||Lpad(I.Brn_No,3,'0')||Lpad(I.Machine_No,5,'0')||Lpad(I.Ad_U_Id,5,'0')||I.Doc_No ;
V_Doc_No := I.Doc_No ;
V_Doc_Ser := I.Doc_Ser ;
Execute Immediate 'Insert Into '||p_yr_br||'.Ias_Deposit_Currency_Mst@'||P_Db_Link||'(
Doc_No, Doc_Ser,Cashier_No,
Cmp_No,Brn_No, Brn_Year, Brn_Usr,PROCESS)
Select '||V_Doc_No||', '||V_Doc_Ser||',Cashier_No,
Doc_Date, Doc_Desc, Machine_No,1,
Cmp_No,Brn_No, Brn_Year, Brn_Usr,PROCESS
Where Doc_No = '||I.Doc_No ||'
And Doc_Ser = '||I.Doc_Ser ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20002,'Error When Insert Into Ias_Deposit_Currency_Mst'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
Execute Immediate 'Insert Into '||p_yr_br||'.Ias_Deposit_Currency_Dtl@'||P_Db_Link||'(
Doc_No, Doc_Ser, Cur_Value, Cur_Number,Ex_Rate,A_Cy,
Select '||V_Doc_No||', '||V_Doc_Ser||', Cur_Value, Cur_Number,Ex_Rate,A_Cy,
Ad_U_Id, Ad_Date, Up_U_Id, Up_Date,
Cmp_No, Brn_No,Brn_Year,Brn_Usr
From Ias_Deposit_Currency_Dtl
Where Doc_No = '||I.Doc_No||'
Raise_Application_Error(-20003,'Error When Insert Into Ias_Deposit_Currency_Dtl'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
Execute Immediate 'Insert Into '||p_yr_br||'.Ias_Deposit_Credit_Card@'||P_Db_Link||'(
Doc_No, Doc_Ser, Cr_Card_No, Cr_Card_Amt, A_Cy, Ex_Rate,
Select '||V_Doc_No||', '||V_Doc_Ser||', Cr_Card_No, Cr_Card_Amt, A_Cy, Ex_Rate,
From Ias_Deposit_Credit_Card
Raise_Application_Error(
-20004,'Error When Insert Into Ias_Deposit_Credit_Card'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
Update Ias_Deposit_Currency_Mst
Set External_Post = 1
Where Exists(Select 1 From Ias_Deposit_Currency_Tmp
Where Ias_Deposit_Currency_Tmp.Doc_Ser = Ias_Deposit_Currency_Mst.Doc_Ser) ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20005,'Error When Update External_Post In Ias_Deposit_Currency_Mst'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
End Move_Cashier_Deposit ;
Procedure Update_Card_Amount(P_Yr_Br In Varchar2) Is
Cursor C_Card_Amount Is Select Card_No, Card_Amt, Rem_Card_Amt,
A_Cy, C_Code, Processed,
Ad_U_Id, Ad_Date, Up_U_Id,
Up_Date, Cmp_No, Brn_No,
Brn_Year, Brn_Usr
Where Exists(Select 1 From Ias_Pos_Card_Amount_Tmp
Where Ias_Pos_Card_Amount_Tmp.Card_No = Ias_Pos_Customer_Card_Amount.Card_No)
Order By Card_No ;
Delete From Ias_Pos_Card_Amount_Tmp Reuse ;
For I_Card_Amount In C_Card_Amount Loop
Execute Immediate 'Update '||P_Yr_Br||'.Ias_Pos_Customer
_Card_Amount@Onyx.Onyx.Com T_Out
Rem_Card_Amt = ( Select Nvl(Card_Amt,0) - Nvl(Rem_Card_Amt,0)
Where Card_No = T_Out.Card_No
And Card_No = '||I_Card_Amount.Card_No||' )
Where Card_No = '||I_Card_Amount.Card_No ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20001,'Error When Update Card Amount In Main Server '||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
Execute Immediate 'Update Ias_Pos_Customer_Card_Amount T_Out
Set Card_Amt = ( Select Nvl(Rem_Card_Amt,0)
From '||P_Yr_Br||'.Ias_Pos_Customer_Card_Amount@Onyx.Onyx.Com
And Card_No = '||I_Card_Amount.Card_No||' ) ,
Update Ias_Pos_Customer_Card_Amount Set Rem_Card_Amt = Nvl(Card_Amt,0)
Where Card_No= I_Card_Amount.Card_No ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20002,'Error When Update Card Amount In Main Server '||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
Update Ias_Pos_Customer_Card_Amount
Where Ias_Pos_Card_Amount_Tmp.Card_No = Ias_Pos_Customer_Card_Amount.Card_No) ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20004,'Error When Update External_Post In Ias_Pos_Customer_Card_Amount'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
End Update_Card_Amount;
Procedure Move_Rt_Bill_Pay_Cash(P_Yr_Br In Varchar2,P_Db_Link In Varchar2) Is
Cursor C_Rt_Bill_Pay_Cash Is Select Ret_Voucher_No, Ret_Voucher_Date,
Rt_Bill_No, Return_Type, A_Cy,
Rt_Bill_Date, Rt_Bill_Rate, Rt_Bill_Amt,
Disc_Amt, Vat_Amt, Cheque_Amt,
Cr_Card_Amt, Machine_No, Ad_U_Id,
Ad_U_Id_Rt_Bill, Ad_Date_Rt_Bill, Machine_No_Rt_Bill,
Bill_No, Bill_Date, Ad_U_Id_Bill,
Ad_Date_Bill, Machine_No_Bill, Pay_Note,
From Ias_Pos_Pay_Cash
Where Exists(Select 1 From Ias_Pos_Pay_Cash_Tmp
Where Ias_Pos_Pay_Cash_Tmp.Rt_Bill_No = Ias_Pos_Pay_Cash.Rt_Bill_No
And Ias_Pos_Pay_Cash_Tmp.Ret_Voucher_No = Ias_Pos_Pay_Cash.Ret_Voucher_No)
Order By Ad_U_Id,Machine_No,Rt_Bill_No,Ret_Voucher_No ;
For I In C_Rt_Bill_Pay_Cash Loop
Execute Immediate 'Insert Into '||p_yr_br||'.Ias_Pos_Pay_Cash@'||P_Db_Link||'(
Ret_Voucher_No, Ret_Voucher_Date,
Ad_Date_Bill, Machine_No_Bill, Pay_Note, External_Post ,
Select Ret_Voucher_No, Ret_Voucher_Date,
Ad_Date_Bill, Machine_No_Bill, Pay_Note,0,
Where Rt_Bill_No = '||I.Rt_Bill_No ||'
And Ret_Voucher_No = '||I.Ret_Voucher_No ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20001,'Error When Insert Into Ias_Pos_Pay_Cash'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
Update Ias_Pos_Pay_Cash
And Ias_Pos_Pay_Cash_Tmp.Ret_Voucher_No = Ias_Pos_Pay_Cash.Ret_Voucher_No) ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20002,'Error When Update External_Post In Ias_Pos_Pay_Cash '||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
End Move_Rt_Bill_Pay_Cash ;
Procedure Insert_Cash_Custmr(p_yr_br In Varchar2,P_Db_Link In Varchar2) Is
Execute Immediate ' Update IAS_POS_CARD set PROCESSED=1 Where crd_no in (Select CUST_CODE From IAS_CASH_CUSTMR
Select CUST_CODE From '||p_yr_br||'.IAS_CASH_CUSTMR@'||P_Db_Link||' ) ';
Execute Immediate ' Insert Into '||p_yr_br||'.IAS_CASH_CUSTMR@'||P_Db_Link||'(
CUST_CODE, CUST_L_NM, CUST_F_NM, CUST_GRP_CODE, MOBILE_NO, POINT_TYP_NO, POINT_TYP_ACTV_DATE, TEL_NO,
FAX_NO, PBOX, E_MAIL, WEB_SITE, R_CODE, CITY_NO, REP_CODE, ADDRESS, BIRTH_DATE, FIRST_DEAL_DATE,
C_BOX_CODE, FIELD1, FIELD2, FIELD3, FIELD4, FIELD5, FIELD6, FIELD7, FIELD8, FIELD9, FIELD10, INACTIVE,
INACTIVE_RES, INACTIVE_DATE, BRN_NO, CMP_NO, AD_U_ID, AD_DATE, UP_U_ID, UP_DATE, PR_REP, UP_CNT,
AD_TRMNL_NM, UP_TRMNL_NM,External_Post)
Select CUST_CODE, CUST_L_NM,
CUST_F_NM, CUST_GRP_CODE, MOBILE_NO, POINT_TYP_NO, POINT_TYP_ACTV_DATE, TEL_NO,
AD_TRMNL_NM, UP_TRMNL_NM,1
From IAS_CASH_CUSTMR Where Nvl(External_Post,0) =0
And CUST_CODE In(Select CUST_CODE From IAS_CASH_CUSTMR
Execute Immediate ' Update IAS_CASH_CUSTMR set External_Post=1 Where Nvl(External_Post,0)=0 ';
--Raise_Application_Error(-20003,'Error When Inserting Cash Custmr '||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
End Insert_Cash_Custmr;
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE Ias_Qt_Prm_Pkg AS
PROCEDURE Ias_Get_Qt_Prm (P_Date In Date,
P_Icode In Varchar2,
P_ItmUnt In Varchar2,
P_Wcode In Number,
P_Iqty In Number,
P_Bill_Amt In Number,
P_Bill_Rate In Number,
P_Bill_Doc_Type In Number Default Null ,
P_Qt_Prm_Type Out Number,
P_Qt_Prm_Method Out Number,
P_Qt_No Out Number,
P_Qt_Ser Out Number,
P_Qt_Icode Out Varchar2,
P_Qt_Itm_Unt Out Varchar2,
P_Qt_Rcrd_No Out Number,
P_Dis_Per Out Number,
P_Dis_Amt Out Number,
P_Price Out Number,
P_Fqty Out Number,
P_Card_Amt Out Number,
P_Qt_Rem_Qty Out Number,
P_No_Of_Dcml In Number) ;
FUNCTION Ias_Get_Qt_Prm_Type ( P_Qt_Ser In Number) RETURN Number;
FUNCTION Ias_Get_Qt_Prm_Method ( P_Qt_Ser In Number) RETURN Number;
FUNCTION Ias_Get_Qt_Prm_Fqty ( P_Qt_Ser In Number , P_Qt_Rcrd_No In Number,P_Icode Varchar2) RETURN Number;
FUNCTION Chk_Qt_Prm_Disc ( P_Qt_Ser In Number ) RETURN Number;
FUNCTION Chk_Qt_Prm_Free_Qty ( P_Qt_Ser In Number ) RETURN Number;
END Ias_Qt_Prm_Pkg;
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY Ias_Qt_Prm_Pkg AS
PROCEDURE Ias_Get_Qt_Prm ( P_Date In Date,
P_Di
s_Per Out Number,
P_No_Of_Dcml In Number) IS
V_Whr Varchar2(3000);
V_Qt_Qty Number;
V_Qt_Qty_Bill Number;
V_Qt_Qty_Bill_H Number;
V_By_Comp_Qty Number;
V_Calc_All_Slides Number;
Select Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Quot_No,
Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Quot_Ser,
Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Type,
Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Method,
Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Qt_I_Code,
Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Qt_Itm_Unt,
Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Rcrd_No,
(Case When Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Method=Decode(Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Type,3,1,2) And Disc_type=1 Then Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Disc_Amt_Per Else Null End),
(Case When Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Method=Decode(Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Type,3,1,2) And Disc_type=2 Then Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Disc_Amt_Per Else Null End),
Nvl(Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.I_Price,0) * Nvl(round(Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Cur_rate(Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Quot_cur)/P_Bill_Rate,P_No_Of_Dcml),0),
Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Free_Qty,
Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Qt_Qty,
Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Card_Amt,
Nvl(By_Comp_Qty,0),
Nvl(Calc_All_Slides,0)
InTo P_Qt_No,
P_Qt_Ser,
P_Qt_Prm_Type,
P_Qt_Prm_Method,
P_Qt_Icode,
P_Qt_Itm_Unt,
P_Qt_Rcrd_No,
P_Dis_Per,
P_Dis_Amt,
P_Price,
P_Fqty,
V_Qt_Qty,
P_Card_Amt,
V_By_Comp_Qty,
V_Calc_All_Slides
From Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst,Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl
Where Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Quot_Ser = Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Quot_Ser
And (Case When Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Type=1 And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.I_Code=P_Icode And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Itm_Unt=P_ItmUnt Then 1
When Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Type=2 And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.I_Code=P_Icode And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Itm_Unt=P_ItmUnt And P_Iqty Between Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.F_Qty And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.T_Qty Then 1
When Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Type=3 And P_Bill_Amt Between Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.F_Amt And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.T_Amt Then 1
Else 0 End)=1
And (Case
When Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Wc_Type=0 Then 1
When Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Wc_Type=1 And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.W_Code = P_Wcode Then 1
When Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Wc_Type=2 And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Whg_Code =(Select Whg_Code From Warehouse_Details Where W_Code=P_Wcode And RowNum<=1) Then 1
When Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Wc_Type=3 And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Cntry_No =(Select Cntry_No From Warehouse_Details Where W_Code=P_Wcode And RowNum<=1) Then 1
When Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Wc_Type=4 And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Prov_No =(Select Prov_No From Warehouse_Details Where W_Code=P_Wcode And RowNum<=1) Then 1
When Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Wc_Type=5 And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.City_No =(Select City_No From Warehouse_Details Where W_Code=P_Wcode And RowNum<=1) Then 1
When Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Wc_Type=6 And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.R_Code =(Select R_Code From Warehouse_Details Where W_Code=P_Wcode And RowNum<=1) Then 1
And Nvl(Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Inactive,0)=0
And P_Bill_Doc_Type = Decode(Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Bill_Doc_Type,Null,P_Bill_Doc_Type,Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Bill_Doc_Type)
And P_date Between F_Date And T_Date
And To_Char(To_Date(P_date),'D') In (Fld_Day1,Fld_Day2,Fld_Day3,Fld_Day4,Fld_Day5,Fld_Day6,Fld_Day7)
And To_Char(Ias_gen_Pkg.Get_Curdate,'HH24:MI:SS') Between Nvl(F_Time,To_Char(Ias_gen_Pkg.Get_Curdate,'HH24:MI:SS')) And Nvl(T_Time,To_Char(Ias_gen_Pkg.Get_Curdate,'HH24:MI:SS'))
--And Decode(Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.W_Code,Null,P_Wcode,Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.W_Code)=P_Wcode
And RowNum <=1 ;
If P_Qt_Ser Is Not Null And P_Qt_Rcrd_No Is Not Null And P_Qt_Prm_Type in (1,2) And P_Qt_Prm_Method<5 Then
If V_Qt_Qty Is Not Null Then
Select Nvl(Sum(Nvl(I_Qty,0)+Nvl(Free_Qty,0)),0) InTo V_Qt_Qty_Bill From Ias_POS_Bill_Dtl Where Qt_Prm_Ser=P_Qt_Ser And Qt_Prm_Rcrd_No=P_Qt_Rcrd_No;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN V_Qt_Qty_Bill:=0;
Select Nvl(Sum(Nvl(I_Qty,0)+Nvl(Free_Qty,0)),0) InTo V_Qt_Qty_Bill_H From Ias_Pos_Hst_Bill_Dtl Where Qt_Prm_Ser=P_Qt_Ser And Qt_Prm_Rcrd_No=P_Qt_Rcrd_No;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN V_Qt_Qty_Bill_H:=0;
V_Qt_Qty_Bill :=Nvl(V_Qt_Qty_Bill,0)+Nvl(V_Qt_Qty_Bill_H,0);
If V_By_Comp_Qty=1 Or V_Calc_All_Slides=1 Then
If P_Qt_Ser Is Not Null And P_Qt_Prm_Type=2 And P_Qt_Prm_Method in(3,4) Then
Select TRUNC(SUM(Case When Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.T_Qty<=P_Iqty And V_Calc_All_Slides=1 Then TRUNC((T_Qty-F_Qty+1)/Decode(V_By_Comp_Qty,1,Comp_Qty,(T_Qty-F_Qty+1)))*Free_Qty
When Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.T_Qty>P_Iqty And V_Calc_All_Slides=1 Then TRUNC((P_Iqty-F_Qty+1)/Decode(V_By_Comp_Qty,1,Comp_Qty,(T_Qty-F_Qty+1)))*Free_Qty
When V_Calc_All_Slides=0 And (P_Iqty Between Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.F_Qty And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.T_Qty) And V_Calc_All_Slides=0 Then TRUNC(TRUNC(P_Iqty/Comp_Qty)*Free_Qty)
End))
InTo P_Fqty
Where Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Quot_Ser =Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Quot_Ser
And Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Quot_Ser =P_Qt_Ser
And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.I_Code =P_Icode
And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Itm_Unt =P_ItmUnt
And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.F_Qty<=P_Iqty
And (Case When Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Qt_Prm_Wc_Type=0 Then 1
Else 0 End)=1;
ElsIf P_Qt_Ser Is Not Null And P_Qt_Prm_Type=2 And P_Qt_Prm_Method=2 And V_Calc_All_Slides=1 Then
Select SUM(Case When Disc_type=1 And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.T_Qty<=P_Iqty Then (((T_Qty-F_Qty+1)*(Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Disc_Amt_Per/100))/P_Iqty)
When Disc_type=1 And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.T_Qty>P_Iqty Then (((P_Iqty-F_Qty+1)*(Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Disc_Amt_Per/100))/P_Iqty)
End),
SUM(Case When Disc_type=2 And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.T_Qty<=P_Iqty Then (((T_Qty-F_Qty+1)*Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Disc_Amt_Per)/P_Iqty)
When Disc_type=2 And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.T_Qty>P_Iqty Then (((P_Iqty-F_Qty+1)*Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Disc_Amt_Per)/P_Iqty)
End)
InTo P_Dis_Per,
P_Dis_Amt
And Ia
s_Qut_Prm_Mst.Quot_Ser = P_Qt_Ser
And Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Itm_Unt=P_ItmUnt
If (V_Qt_Qty Is Not Null And (P_Iqty> (Nvl(V_Qt_Qty,0)-Nvl(V_Qt_Qty_Bill,0)))) Or (P_Qt_Prm_Method=4 And V_By_Comp_Qty=1 And P_Fqty=0) Then
P_Qt_Rem_Qty := (Nvl(V_Qt_Qty,0)-Nvl(V_Qt_Qty_Bill,0)) ;
P_Qt_No := Null;
P_Qt_Ser := Null;
P_Qt_Icode := Null;
P_Qt_Itm_Unt := Null;
P_Qt_Rcrd_No := Null;
P_Dis_Per := Null;
P_Dis_Amt := Null;
P_Price := Null;
P_Fqty := Null;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL;
END Ias_Get_Qt_Prm ;
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FUNCTION Ias_Get_Qt_Prm_Type ( P_Qt_Ser In Number) RETURN Number Is
V_Qt_Prm_Type Number;
Select Qt_Prm_Type
Into v_Qt_Prm_Type
From ias_qut_prm_mst
Where quot_ser = P_qt_ser
Return(v_Qt_Prm_Type);
End Ias_Get_Qt_Prm_Type;
FUNCTION Ias_Get_Qt_Prm_Method ( P_Qt_Ser In Number) RETURN Number Is
V_Qt_Prm_Method Number;
Select Qt_Prm_Method
Into v_Qt_Prm_Method
Return(v_Qt_Prm_Method);
End Ias_Get_Qt_Prm_Method;
FUNCTION Ias_Get_Qt_Prm_Fqty ( P_Qt_Ser In Number , P_Qt_Rcrd_No In Number,P_Icode Varchar2) RETURN Number Is
V_Qt_Fqty Number;
If P_qt_ser Is Not Null And P_qt_Rcrd_No Is Not Null Then
Select Free_Qty
Into v_Qt_Fqty
From ias_qut_prm_dtl
and rcrd_no = P_Qt_Rcrd_No
If Nvl(v_Qt_Fqty,0)=0 Then
From ias_qut_prm_sub_dtl
and i_code=P_Icode
Return(Nvl(v_Qt_Fqty,0));
End Ias_Get_Qt_Prm_Fqty;
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FUNCTION Chk_Qt_Prm_Disc ( P_Qt_Ser In Number ) RETURN Number IS
V_Use_Disc Number;
If P_qt_ser Is Not Null Then
Select Disc_Amt_Per
Into V_Use_Disc
Return(V_Use_Disc);
End Chk_Qt_Prm_Disc;
FUNCTION Chk_Qt_Prm_Free_Qty ( P_Qt_Ser In Number ) RETURN Number IS
V_Use_Fqty Number;
Into V_Use_Fqty
If Nvl(V_Use_Fqty,0)=0 Then
Return(V_Use_Fqty);
End Chk_Qt_Prm_Free_Qty;
End Ias_Qt_Prm_Pkg;

CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE Body IAS_Usr_Pkg as
FUNCTION Get_Usr_Nm (P_Usr_No In USER_R.U_ID%TYPE,P_Lng_no In Number Default 1 ) Return VARCHAR2 Is
Select Decode(P_Lng_no,1,u_a_name,nvl(u_e_name,u_a_name))
where U_id = P_Usr_No
End Get_Usr_Nm ;
FUNCTION Get_Usr_St (P_Usr_No In USER_R.U_ID%TYPE) Return NUMBER Is
v_inc
Into v_inc
Return(v_inc);
End Get_Usr_St ;
FUNCTION Get_Usr_Def_Val ( P_Usr_No In USER_R.U_ID%TYPE , P_Def_Type In Varchar2) Return VARCHAR2 Is
v_val varchar2(30);
-- P_Def_Type = W_CODE , Cash_No
If P_Def_Type = 'W_CODE' Then
Select W_CODE_DEF
Into v_val
where U_Id = P_Usr_No
and Exists (Select 1 From Privilege_wh
Where U_id = P_Usr_No
and w_code=User_r.W_CODE_DEF
and add_flag = 1
and RowNum<=1)
ElsIf P_Def_Type = 'CASH_NO' Then
Select Cash_No_DEF
and Exists (Select 1 From Priv_Cash
and cash_no=User_r.CASH_NO_DEF
cash_type = 1
Return(v_val);
End Get_Usr_Def_Val ;
FUNCTION Chk_Usr_Frm_Prv (P_UserNo In Number , P_Frm_no In Number, P_Priv_Type In Varchar2) RETURN NUMBER Is
v_Flg Number;
If Upper(P_Priv_Type) = 'INC' Then
ElsIf Upper(P_Priv_Type) = 'ADD' Then
Select nvl(Ad_Flag,0)
ElsIf Upper(P_Priv_Type) = 'MOD' Then
Select nvl(Mod_Flag,0)
ElsIf Upper(P_Priv_Type) = 'DEL' Then
Select nvl(Del_Flag,0)
ElsIf Upper(P_Priv_Type) = 'VIEW' Then
Select nvl(View_Flag,0)
From PRIVILEGE
ElsIf Upper(P_Priv_Type) = 'PRINT' Then
Select nvl(Print_Flag,0)
ElsIf Upper(P_Priv_Type) = 'VWREP' Then
Select nvl(VWREP_FLAG,0)
End Chk_Usr_Frm_Prv;
FUNCTION Chk_Usr_CB_Prv (P_UserNo In Number , P_Cb_Type In Number ,P_Cb_No In Number, P_a_cy In Varchar2,P_Priv_Type In Varchar2) RETURN NUMBER Is
If Upper(P_Priv_Type) = 'ADD' Then
Select nvl(Add_Flag,0)
From Priv_Cash
and Cash_no = P_Cb_No
and A_cy = P_a_cy
and Cash_Type = P_Cb_Type
End Chk_Usr_CB_Prv;
FUNCTION Chk_Usr_Cst_Prv (P_UserNo In Number , P_c_code In VARCHAR2, P_a_cy In VARCHAR2,P_Priv_Type In Varchar2) RETURN NUMBER Is
From IAS_PRIV_CUSTOMER
and c_code = P_c_code
End Chk_Usr_Cst_Prv;
FUNCTION Chk_Usr_Wc_Prv (P_UserNo In Number , P_W_code In NUMBER,P_Priv_Type In Varchar2) RETURN NUMBER Is
From PRIVILEGE_WH
and W_code = P_W_code
End Chk_Usr_Wc_Prv;
FUNCTION Chk_Usr_Gc_Prv (P_UserNo In
Number , P_G_code In Varchar2,P_Priv_Type In Varchar2) RETURN NUMBER Is
From PRIVILEGE_Gc
and G_code = P_G_code
End Chk_Usr_Gc_Prv;
FUNCTION Chk_Usr_Show_Prv(P_UserNo In Number ,P_T_U_ID In NUMBER) RETURN NUMBER Is
Select nvl(PRIV_FLAG,0)
From IAS_SHW_DOC_PRIV
and T_U_ID = P_T_U_ID
End Chk_Usr_Show_Prv;
FUNCTION Get_Usr_Def_Rep ( P_Usr_No In USER_R.U_ID%TYPE , P_Form_no In FORM_DETAIL.FORM_NO%TYPE ) Return NUMBER Is
v_def Number;
Select REP_SMPLE_NO
Into v_def
From IAS_REP_SMPLE_USER
and Form_no = P_Form_no
and REP_SMPLE_DFLT = 1
and nvl(PRIV_FLAG,0)= 1
Return (v_def);
END Get_Usr_Def_Rep;
FUNCTION Chk_Usr_Brn_Prv (P_UserNo In Number , P_Brn_No In Number,P_Priv_Type In Varchar2) RETURN NUMBER Is
and Brn_No = P_Brn_No
--------------------
FUNCTION CHK_USR ( P_USR_NO IN USER_R.U_ID%TYPE,P_PASSWORD IN VARCHAR2 ,P_LNG_NO IN NUMBER DEFAULT 1) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
V_Inactive User_R.Inactive%Type;
V_Pwd User_R.Password%Type;
V_S_Sdate User_R.S_Sdate%Type;
V_S_Edate User_R.S_Edate%Type;
SELECT NVL(INACTIVE,0) ,
PASSWORD ,
S_SDATE,
S_EDATE
INTO V_INACTIVE,
V_PWD,
V_S_SDATE,
V_S_EDATE
FROM USER_R
WHERE U_ID = P_USR_NO
IF V_PWD <> Ias_Get_Enc_Pass_fnc( P_Pass=>P_PASSWORD) THEN
RETURN(IAS_GEN_PKG.GET_MSG(P_LNG_NO =>P_LNG_NO,P_MSG_NO=>452));
ELSIF V_INACTIVE =1 THEN
RETURN(IAS_GEN_PKG.GET_MSG(P_LNG_NO =>P_LNG_NO,P_MSG_NO=>380 ));
ELSIF V_S_SDATE IS NOT NULL AND V_S_EDATE IS NOT NULL AND SYSDATE NOT BETWEEN V_S_SDATE AND V_S_EDATE THEN
RETURN(750);
RETURN(IAS_GEN_PKG.GET_MSG(P_LNG_NO =>P_LNG_NO, P_MSG_NO=>750 ));
RETURN(IAS_GEN_PKG.GET_MSG(P_LNG_NO =>P_LNG_NO, P_MSG_NO=>453 ));
END CHK_USR;

CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE IAS_CshBnk_Pkg as
FUNCTION Get_CB_Name (P_Cb_Type In Number,P_Cb_No In Number,P_Lng_no In Number) RETURN VARCHAR2;
FUNCTION Get_CB_No_By_Nm (P_Cb_Type In Number,P_cb_nm In ACCOUNT.A_NAME%TYPE) RETURN VARCHAR2;
FUNCTION Get_A_code (P_Cb_Type In Number,P_Cb_No In Number) RETURN VARCHAR2;
FUNCTION Check_Acy (P_Cb_Type In Number,P_Cb_No In Number,P_a_cy In VARCHAR2) RETURN Number;
FUNCTION Get_CB_Ser (P_Cb_Type In Number,P_Cb_No In Number) RETURN Number;
FUNCTION Get_Csh_Type(P_Csh_No In CASH_IN_HAND.CASH_NO%TYPE) RETURN Number;
FUNCTION Get_CB_No (P_Cb_Type In Number , P_a_code ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE, P_Acy In EX_RATE.CUR_CODE%TYPE DEFAULT NULL) RETURN NUMBER;
FUNCTION Check_Mediator_Bnk (P_Cb_No In Number) RETURN Number;
FUNCTION Chk_Ac_Csh_Bnk (P_a_code ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE) RETURN NUMBER;
FUNCTION Get_Cnt_Acy (P_Cb_Type In Number ,P_Cb_No In Number,P_Usr_No In Number) RETURN NUMBER;
FUNCTION Get_One_Acy_Cb (P_Cb_Type In Number ,P_Cb_No In Number,P_Usr_no In Number) RETURN VARCHAR2;
FUNCTION Get_CB_Brn (P_Cb_Type In Number ,P_Cb_No In Number) RETURN NUMBER;
FUNCTION Chk_CB_Brn (P_Cb_Type In Number ,P_Cb_No In Number,P_Brn_no In Number) RETURN NUMBER;
End IAS_CshBnk_Pkg;
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE Body IAS_CshBnk_Pkg as
--================================================
================
FUNCTION Get_CB_Name (P_Cb_Type In Number,P_Cb_No In Number,P_Lng_no In Number) RETURN VARCHAR2 Is
If P_Cb_Type = 1 Then -- Cash
Select Decode(P_Lng_no,1,CASH_NAME,nvl(CASH_E_NAME,CASH_NAME))
From Cash_In_hand
where Cash_no=P_Cb_No ;
ElsIf P_Cb_Type = 2 Then -- Bank
Select Decode(P_Lng_no,1,BANK_NAME,nvl(BANK_E_NAME,BANK_NAME))
From Cash_At_Bank
where bank_no=P_Cb_No ;
End Get_CB_Name ;
FUNCTION Get_CB_No_By_Nm (P_Cb_Type In Number,P_cb_nm In ACCOUNT.A_NAME%TYPE) RETURN VARCHAR2 Is
v_cb_no NUMBER(15);
IF P_Cb_Type = 1 THEN
select Cash_no
Into v_cb_no
where (cash_name = P_cb_nm or cash_e_name =P_cb_nm )
select Bank_no
where (bank_name = P_cb_nm or bank_e_name =P_cb_nm )
RETURN(v_cb_no);
End Get_CB_No_By_Nm ;
FUNCTION Check_Acy (P_Cb_Type In Number,P_Cb_No In Number,P_a_cy In VARCHAR2) RETURN Number Is
From Ias_Cash_In_Hand_Dtl
where Cash_no=P_Cb_No
From Ias_Cash_At_Bank_Dtl
where Bank_no=P_Cb_No
FUNCTION Get_CB_Ser (P_Cb_Type In Number,P_Cb_No In Number) RETURN Number Is
v_sr Number;
Select nvl(cash_sr,0)
Into v_sr
From Cash_in_Hand
where Cash_no=P_Cb_No;
Select nvl(Bank_sr,0)
From Cash_at_bank
where Bank_no=P_Cb_No;
RETURN(v_sr);
End Get_CB_Ser;
FUNCTION Get_A_code (P_Cb_Type In Number,P_Cb_No In Number) RETURN VARCHAR2 Is
v_a_code Number;
Select A_code
Into v_a_code
RETURN(v_a_code);
End Get_A_code;
FUNCTION Get_Csh_Type(P_Csh_No In CASH_IN_HAND.CASH_NO%TYPE) RETURN Number Is
v_csh_type Number;
Select Cash_Type
Into v_csh_type
where Cash_no=P_Csh_No;
RETURN(v_csh_type);
End Get_Csh_Type;
FUNCTION Get_CB_No (P_Cb_Type In Number , P_a_code ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE , P_Acy In EX_RATE.CUR_CODE%TYPE DEFAULT NULL) RETURN NUMBER Is
v_cb_no Number;
Select Cash_No
Where A_code = P_a_code;
Select Bank_No
From Cash_at_Bank
If P_Acy Is not Null Then
Select Cash_no
From Ias_Cash_in_Hand_Dtl
Where A_code = P_a_code
and A_cy = P_Acy;
Select Bank_no
From Ias_Cash_at_Bank_Dtl
End Get_CB_No;
FUNCTION Check_Mediator_Bnk (P_Cb_No In Number) RETURN Number Is
v_md Number:=0;
If P_Cb_No Is Not Null Then
Select nvl(MEDIATOR,0)
Into v_md
where Bank_no = P_Cb_No
RETURN(v_md);
End Check_Mediator_Bnk;
FUNCTION Chk_Ac_Csh_Bnk (P_a_code ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE) RETURN NUMBER Is
v_typ NUMBER;
--v_typ = 1 Cash
--v_typ = 2 Bank
If P_a_code Is Not Null Then --(1)
Select 1 Into Cnt From Cash_in_Hand
Where a_code =P_a_code
If Cnt = 0 Then
Select 1 Into Cnt From Cash_At_Bank
If Cnt>0 Then
v_typ:=2; -- bank
v_typ:=0;-- No
v_typ:=1; -- cash
Return(v_typ);
END Chk_Ac_Csh_Bnk;
FUNCTION Get_Cnt_Acy (P_Cb_Type In Number ,P_Cb_No In Number,P_Usr_No In Number) RETURN NUMBER Is
If P_Cb_Type = 1 Then
From IAS_CASH_IN_HAND_DTL
Where Cash_No =P_Cb_No
And A_Cy In (Select A_Cy
Where Cash_Type=P_Cb_Type
And U_Id = P_Usr_No
And Cash_No= P_Cb_No And Add_Flag=1);
ElsIf P_Cb_Type = 2 Then
From IAS_CASH_AT_BANK_DTL
Where Bank_No =P_Cb_No
FUNCTION Get_One_Acy_Cb (P_Cb_Type In Number ,P_Cb_No In Number,P_Usr_no In Number) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
v_Acy varchar2(7);
Into v_Acy
And Cash_No= P_Cb_No And Add_Flag=1 ;
RETURN(v_Acy);
End Get_One_Acy_Cb ;
--===================================================================
FUNCTION Get_CB_Brn (P_Cb_Type In Number ,P_Cb_No In Number) RETURN NUMBER Is
v_brn Number;
Select conn_brn_no
Into v_brn
where cash_no = P_Cb_No
where bank_no = P_Cb_No
Return(v_brn);
End Get_CB_Brn ;
UNCTION Chk_CB_Brn (P_Cb_Type In Number ,P_Cb_No In Number,P_Brn_no In Number) RETURN NUMBER Is
v_cnt Number:=0;
and conn_brn_no = P_Brn_no
End Chk_CB_Brn ;

PROCEDURE Get_Credit_Lmt (p_cr_lmt_typ In NUMBER,
P_local_cur In Varchar2,
P_frc_no In Number
Default 2,
p_cstlmtper OUT NUMBER) IS
v_crtype Number:=0;
Select nvl(crlimit_type,1)
Into v_crtype
Where u_id=P_User_No;
v_crtype:=1;
-- crlimit_type 1= not allow ,2=allow ,3=allow with worning
IF P_CR_LMT_TYP = 2 THEN -- BY COST CENTER
SELECT ROUND (NVL (CR_LIMIT, 0), P_FRC_NO), NVL (INV_LIMIT, 0),
CST_LMT_TYP, CST_LMT_PER
INTO P_CRLMT, P_INVLMT,
P_CSTLMTTYP, P_CSTLMTPER
FROM IAS_CUSTOMER_CC_LMT
WHERE C_CODE = P_C_CODE AND A_CY = P_ACY AND CC_CODE = P_CC_CODE;
FROM CUSTOMER_CURR
WHERE C_CODE = P_C_CODE AND A_CY = P_ACY;
P_CRLMT := 0;
P_INVLMT := 0;
P_CSTLMTTYP := 2;
P_CSTLMTPER := NULL;
End Get_Credit_Lmt;
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE IAS_PRMTR_PKG IS
G_BRN_YEAR NUMBER(4);
G_USER_NO USER_R.U_ID%TYPE;
G_LANG_NO NUMBER;
G_LOCAL_CUR EX_RATE.CUR_CODE%TYPE;
FUNCTION GetPval (P_Prmtr VARCHAR2) RETURN VARCHAR2;
PROCEDURE SetPval(P_Prmtr VARCHAR2, P_Val VARCHAR2);
END IAS_PRMTR_PKG ;
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY IAS_PRMTR_PKG IS
FUNCTION GetPval(p_prmtr VARCHAR2) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
If 'G_BRN_YEAR' ='G_'||p_prmtr Then
RETURN IAS_PRMTR_PKG.G_BRN_YEAR ;
ElsIf 'G_USER_NO' ='G_'||p_prmtr Then
RETURN IAS_PRMTR_PKG.G_USER_NO;
ElsIf 'G_LANG_NO' ='G_'||p_prmtr Then
RETURN IAS_PRMTR_PKG.G_LANG_NO ;
ElsIf 'G_LOCAL_CUR' ='G_'||p_pr
mtr Then
RETURN IAS_PRMTR_PKG.G_LOCAL_CUR ;
RETURN '';
END GetPval;
/*_____________________________________________________________________*/
PROCEDURE SetPval(p_prmtr VARCHAR2,p_val VARCHAR2) IS
IF 'G_BRN_YEAR' ='G_'||p_prmtr Then
IAS_PRMTR_PKG.G_BRN_YEAR :=to_number (p_val) ;
ELSIF 'G_USER_NO' ='G_'||p_prmtr Then
IAS_PRMTR_PKG.G_USER_NO:=to_number (p_val) ;
ELSIF 'G_LANG_NO' ='G_'||p_prmtr Then
IAS_PRMTR_PKG.G_LANG_NO :=to_number (p_val) ;
ElsIf 'G_LOC_CUR' ='G_'||p_prmtr Then
IAS_PRMTR_PKG.G_LOCAL_CUR := (p_val) ;
END SetPval;
END IAS_PRMTR_PKG;

CREATE OR REPLACE Package Pos_Point_Pkg As

CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY Pos_Point_Pkg AS
Function Get_Cust_Nm( P_Cust_Code In Ias_Cash_Custmr.Cust_Code%Type,P_Lng_No In Number) Return Varchar2 Is
V_Cust_Name Ias_Cash_Custmr.Cust_L_Nm%Type;
Select Decode(P_Lng_No,1,Cust_L_Nm,Nvl(Cust_F_Nm,Cust_L_Nm))
Into V_Cust_Name
From Ias_Cash_Custmr
Where Cust_Code=P_Cust_Code
Return(V_Cust_Name);
End Get_Cust_Nm;
Function Check_Cust_Inactv (P_Cust_Code In Ias_Cash_Custmr.Cust_Code%Type) Return Number Is
V_Inactv Number;
Select Nvl(Inactive,0)
Into V_Inactv
Return(V_Inactv);
End Check_Cust_Inactv;
Function Get_Cust_Point_Typ( P_Cust_Code In Ias_Cash_Custmr.Cust_Code%Type) Return Number Is
V_Point_typ_no Ias_Point_Typ_Mst.Point_Typ_No%Type;
Select Point_Typ_No
Into V_Point_typ_no
Return(V_Point_typ_no);
End Get_Cust_Point_Typ;
Function Get_Point_Type_Nm(P_Point_Typ_No In Ias_Point_Typ_Mst.Point_Typ_No%Type,P_Lng_No In Number) Return Varchar2 Is
V_Type_Name I
as_Point_Typ_Mst.Typ_l_Nm%Type;
Select Decode(P_Lng_No,1,Typ_L_Nm,Nvl(Typ_F_Nm,Typ_L_Nm))
Into V_Type_Name
From Ias_Point_Typ_Mst
Where Point_Typ_No=P_Point_Typ_No
Return(V_Type_Name);
End Get_Point_Type_Nm;
Function Check_Point_Type_State (P_Point_Typ_No In Ias_Point_Typ_Mst.Point_Typ_No%Type) Return Number Is
V_State Number :=0;
Into V_State
If Nvl(V_State,0)=1 Then
V_State:=1637;
V_State:=0;
If Nvl(V_State,0)=0 Then
And To_Date(Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Curdate,'DD/MM/YYYY') Between Start_Date And End_Date
V_State:=2222;
Return(V_State);
End Check_Point_Type_State;
P_Min_Lmt_Rplc Out Number) Is
Select Min_Lmt_Calc,
Min_Lmt_Rplc
Into P_Min_Lmt_Calc,
P_Min_Lmt_Rplc
Where Point_Typ_No=P_Point_Typ_No;
End Get_Point_Typ_Min_Lmt ;
Function Get_Point_Calc_Typ(P_Point_Typ_No In Ias_Point_Typ_Mst.Point_Typ_No%Type) Return Number Is
V_Calc_Typ Number(1);
Select Point_Calc_Typ
Into V_Calc_Typ
Return(V_Calc_Typ);
urn(null);
End Get_Point_Calc_Typ;
Function Get_Point_Cnt(P_Point_Typ_No In Ias_Point_Typ_Mst.Point_Typ_No%Type,P_Bill_Amt Number) Return Number Is
V_Point_Cnt Number;
V_Point_Calc_Typ Number;
V_Amt_4Point Number;
V_Point_Calc_Typ:=Pos_Point_Pkg.Get_Point_Calc_Typ(P_Point_Typ_No);
If V_Point_Calc_Typ =1 Then
Select Amt_4Point Into V_Amt_4Point
V_Amt_4Point:=0;
V_Point_Cnt:=trunc(P_Bill_Amt / V_Amt_4Point,0);
Elsif V_Point_Calc_Typ=2 Then
Select Amt_4Point,Point_Cnt Into V_Amt_4Point,V_Point_Cnt
Where Point_Typ_No= P_Point_Typ_No;
V_Point_Cnt :=0;
V_Point_Cnt:=trunc(P_Bill_Amt / V_Amt_4Point,0)*V_Point_Cnt;
Elsif V_Point_Calc_Typ=3 Then
Select Point_Cnt Into V_Point_Cnt
From Ias_Point_Typ_Calc_Dtl
Where Point_Typ_No= P_Point_Typ_No
And P_Bill_Amt Between From_Amt And To_Amt;
V_Point_Cnt:=0;
Return(V_Point_Cnt);
End Get_Point_Cnt;
Function Get_Point_Rplc_Cnt(P_Point_Typ_No In Ias_Point_Typ_Mst.Point_Typ_No%Type,P_Amt_Rplc Number) Return Number Is
V_Point_Rplc_Cnt Number;
V_Point_Clc_Rlc_Typ Number;
V_AMT_4POINT_RPLC Number;
V_Point_Clc_Rlc_Typ:=Pos_Point_Pkg.Get_Point_Calc_Typ(P_Point_Typ_No);
If V_Point_Clc_Rlc_Typ =1 Then
Select AMT_4POINT_RPLC Into V_AMT_4POINT_RPLC
V_AMT_4POINT_RPLC:=0;
V_Point_Rplc_Cnt:=trunc(P_Amt_Rplc / V_AMT_4POINT_RPLC,0);
Elsif V_Point_Clc_Rlc_Typ=2 Then
Select AMT_4POINT_RPLC,POINT_CNT_RPLC Into V_AMT_4POINT_RPLC,V_Point_Rplc_Cnt
V_Point_Rplc_Cnt :=0;
V_Point_Rplc_Cnt:=trunc(P_Amt_Rplc / V_AMT_4POINT_RPLC,0)*V_Point_Rplc_Cnt;
Elsif V_Point_Clc_Rlc_Typ=3 Then
SELECT TO_POINT INTO V_Point_Rplc_Cnt
FROM
Select TO_POINT
From IAS_POINT_TYP_RPLC_DTL
And AMT<=P_Amt_Rplc
ORDER BY TO_POINT DESC
WHERE ROWNUM<=1 ;
V_Point_Rplc_Cnt:=0;
Return(V_Point_Rplc_Cnt);
End Get_Point_Rplc_Cnt;
Function Get_Point_Amt(P_Point_Typ_No In Ias_Point_Typ_Mst.Point_Typ_No%Type,P_Point_cnt Number) Return Number Is
V_Point_Clc_Typ Number;
V_Amt Number;
V_Point_Clc_Typ:=Pos_Point_Pkg.Get_Point_Calc_Typ(P_Point_Typ_No);
If V_Point_Clc_Typ =1 Then
V_Amt:=P_Point_cnt*V_Amt_4Point;
Elsif V_Point_Clc_Typ=2 Then
V_Amt:=(V_Amt_4Poin
t/V_Point_Cnt)*P_Point_cnt;
Elsif V_Point_Clc_Typ=3 Then
Select Amt Into V_Amt
And P_Point_cnt Between From_Point And To_Point;
V_Amt:=0;
Return(V_Amt);
End Get_Point_Amt;
Function Get_Point_Rplc_Amt(P_Point_Typ_No In Ias_Point_Typ_Mst.Point_Typ_No%Type,P_Point_cnt Number) Return Number Is
V_Amt_4Point_rplc Number;
V_Point_Cnt_rplc Number;
V_Amt_Rplc Number;
Select Amt_4Point_rplc Into V_Amt_4Point_rplc
V_Amt_4Point_rplc:=0;
V_Amt_Rplc:=P_Point_cnt*V_Amt_4Point_rplc;
Select Amt_4Point_rplc,Point_Cnt_rplc Into V_Amt_4Point_rplc,V_Point_Cnt_rplc
V_Point_Cnt_rplc :=0;
V_Amt_Rplc:=(V_Amt_4Point_rplc/V_Point_Cnt_rplc)*P_Point_cnt;
Select Amt Into V_Amt_Rplc
V_Amt_Rplc:=0;
Return(V_Amt_Rplc);
End Get_Point_Rplc_Amt;
Function Get_Cust_Point_bal(P_Cust_Code In Ias_Cash_Custmr.Cust_Code%Type,
P_Point_Aprvd_By_Day In Number Default Null,
P_Lng_No In Number default 1) Return number Is
V_Whr Varchar2(1000);
V_Point_cnt Number;
V_Point_Amt_Bal Number;
V_Whr:=' and To_Date(trns_date,''DD/MM/YYYY'')<= Decode(Trns_Type,1,To_Date(Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Curdate,''DD/MM/YYYY'')-'||Nvl(P_Point_Aprvd_By_Day,0)||',To_Date(Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Curdate,''DD/MM/YYYY''))';
If P_Db_Link Is Null Then
If P_Point_Typ_No Is Not Null Then
Select Sum(Nvl(Point_Cnt,0)),Sum(Nvl(Doc_Amt,0))
Into V_Point_cnt ,V_Point_Amt_Bal
From Pos_Point_Calc_Trns
And Cust_Code = P_Cust_Code
and To_Date(trns_date,'DD/MM/YYYY')<= Decode(Trns_Type,1,To_Date(Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Curdate,'DD/MM/YYYY')-Nvl(P_Point_Aprvd_By_Day,0),To_Date(Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Curdate,'DD/MM/YYYY'));
Into V_Point_cnt,V_Point_Amt_Bal
Where Cust_Code = P_Cust_Code
Execute Immediate ' Select Sum(Nvl(Point_Cnt,0)),Sum(Nvl(Doc_Amt,0)) From '||User||'.Pos_Point_Calc_Trns@'||P_
Db_Link||'
Where Point_Typ_No ='|| P_Point_Typ_No||
' And Cust_Code ='|| P_Cust_Code ||
V_Whr
Into V_Point_cnt,V_Point_Amt_Bal ;
V_Point_cnt := 0 ;
Raise_application_error(-2001,Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(P_Lng_No,2225) ||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
Execute Immediate ' Select Sum(Nvl(Point_Cnt,0)),Sum(Nvl(Doc_Amt,0)) From '||User||'.Pos_Point_Calc_Trns@'||P_Db_Link||'
Where Cust_Code ='|| P_Cust_Code ||V_Whr
Select Point_Calc_Typ Into V_Point_Calc_Typ
V_Point_Calc_Typ:=0;
If Nvl(V_Point_Calc_Typ,0)=1 Then
If Nvl(V_Point_Amt_Bal,0)>0 And P_Point_Typ_No is Not Null Then
V_Point_cnt:=Pos_Point_Pkg.Get_Point_Cnt(P_Point_Typ_No=>P_Point_Typ_No,
P_Bill_Amt =>V_Point_Amt_Bal);
End Get_Cust_Point_bal;
Procedure Check_Insert_Expired_Point(P_Ad_U_Id Number Default Null,P_Brn_No Number Default Null,P_Machine_No Number Default Null ) Is
V_Point_Age_Expired Number;
V_Point_Amt Number;
V_Last_Expire_Date Date;
Select POINT_AGE_EXPIRED_TYP Into V_Point_Age_Expired From Ias_Para_Pos;
If V_Point_Age_Expired=0 Then
If To_Char(Ias_Gen_Pkg.
Get_Final_Day,'DD/MM/YYYY')>= To_Char(Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Curdate,'DD/MM/YYYY') Then
Cursor C_Cust Is Select Cust_code,MOBILE_NO
Where To_Char(Trns_Date,'DD/MM/YYYY') <=To_Char(Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Final_Day,'DD/MM/YYYY')
Having Nvl(Sum(POINT_CNT),0)>0
Group by Cust_code,MOBILE_NO
Order By Cust_Code;
For I_Cust In C_Cust Loop
Select Sum(POINT_CNT),Sum(DOC_AMT) into V_Point_Cnt,V_Point_Amt
Where Cust_Code =I_Cust.Cust_code;
Exception When Others then
If Nvl(V_Point_Cnt,0)>0 Then
Insert Into Pos_Point_Calc_trns (TRNS_NO,
TRNS_DATE,
CUST_CODE,
MOBILE_NO,
POINT_TYP_NO,
Bill_NO,
DOC_AMT,
POINT_CNT,
TRNS_TYPE,
machine_no,
AD_U_ID,
AD_DATE,
BRN_NO,
CMP_NO,
--BRN_YEAR,
BRN_USR )
Values(POS_POINT_SEQ.
NEXTVAL,
To_Char(Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Sysdate,'DD/MM/YYYY') ,
I_Cust.Cust_code,
I_Cust.MOBILE_NO,
Pos_Point_Pkg.Get_Cust_Point_typ(I_Cust.Cust_code),
NULL ,
-V_Point_Cnt,
To_Char(Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Sysdate,'DD/MM/YYYY'),
IAS_Brn_Pkg.Get_Br_Cmp(P_BRN_NO),
To_Number(To_Char(Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Sysdate,'YYYY'))
Elsif V_Point_Age_Expired>0 Then
Cursor C_Cust
Is Select Cust_code,MOBILE_NO
Where ADD_MONTHS (To_Char(FIRST_DEAL_DATE,'DD/MM/YYYY'),12*V_Point_Age_Expired) < To_Char(Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Sysdate,'DD/MM/YYYY')
Select Sum(POINT_CNT) into V_Point_Cnt
Select max(trns_date) into V_Last_Expire_Date
Where Cust_Code =I_Cust.Cust_code
And trns_type=9;
V_Last_Expire_Date:=Null;
If Nvl(V_Point_Cnt,0)> 0 And ( V_Last_Expire_Date Is Null Or
ADD_MONTHS (To_Char(V_Last_Expire_Date,'DD/MM/YYYY'),12*V_Point_Age_Expired) < To_Char(Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Sysdate,'DD/MM/YYYY') ) Then
BRN_YEAR,
Values(POS_POINT_SEQ.NEXTVAL,
NULL,
To_Number(To_Char(Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Sysdate,'YYYY')),
Raise_application_error(-2001,Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(1,2225) ||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
End Check_Insert_Expired_Point;

--##--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------##--
FUNCTION GET_MSG_TXT_FNC ( P_Msgx_Txt In Varchar2 ) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
V_TXT varchar2(4000);
Execute Immediate ( P_Msgx_Txt) Into V_TXT ;
Return(V_TXT);
Return(NULL);
END GET_MSG_TXT_FNC;
P_LNG_NO IN NUMBER DEFAULT 1,
P_DOC_TYP
IN NUMBER DEFAULT NULL,
P_DOC_SRL
P_AD_DATE
IN DATE DEFAULT SYSDATE,
P_CMP_NO
P_BRN_NO
P_BRN_USR
P_BRN_YEAR
IN NUMBER DEFAULT NULL) IS
V_SR_NO NUMBER;
V_SR_SRL NUMBER:=0;
SELECT NVL(MAX(SR_NO),0) + 1
INTO V_SR_NO
FROM IAS_SYS.S_MSG_MOBILE_AUTO
WHERE BRN_USR = NVL(P_BRN_USR,G_BRN_USR)
AND BRN_YEAR = NVL(P_BRN_YEAR,G_BRN_YEAR);
V_SR_NO :=1;
IF V_SR_NO IS NOT NULL THEN
V_SR_SRL:= NVL(P_BRN_YEAR,G_BRN_YEAR)||LPAD(NVL(P_BRN_USR,G_BRN_USR),3,0)||V_SR_NO;
IF NVL(V_SR_SRL,0) > 0 AND P_MSG_TYP IS NOT NULL AND P_MSG_TXT IS NOT NULL AND ( P_MOBILE_NO IS NOT NULL OR P_E_MAIL IS NOT NULL ) THEN
INSERT INTO IAS_SYS.S_MSG_MOBILE_AUTO
( LNG_NO,
MSG_MTHD_SEND,
SR_NO,
SR_SRL,
MSG_TYP,
MSG_TXT,
TO_EMAIL,
EMAIL_SUBJECT,
DOC_TYP,
DOC_SRL,
PROCSSD_EMAIL_FLG,
PROCSSD_SMS_FLG,
MSG_DATE,
BRN_USR,
BRN_YEAR)
VALUES( NVL(P_LNG_NO,1),
P_MSG_MTHD_SEND,
V_SR_NO,
V_SR_SRL,
P_MSG_TYP,
P_MSG_TXT,
P_MOBILE_NO,
P_E_MAIL,
P_SUBJCT_MAIL,
P_DOC_TYP,
P_DOC_SRL,
P_AD_DATE,
SYSDATE,
P_CMP_NO,
P_BRN_NO,
NVL(P_BRN_USR,G_BRN_USR),
NVL(P_BRN_YEAR,G_BRN_YEAR) );
RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20000,'ERROR WHEN INSE
RT S_MSG_MOBILE_AUTO = '||SQLERRM);
END INSRT_MSG_ALRT_PRC;
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE UPDATE_RT_BILL_PAY_RPLC_SRVR IS
Update IAS_POS_RT_BILL_MST SET PAYED =1
WHERE RT_BILL_NO IN (
select rt_bill_no from IAS_POS_RT_BILL_MST M WHERE PAYED=0
AND EXISTS (SELECT RT_BILL_NO FROM IAS_POS_PAY_BILLS WHERE IAS_POS_PAY_BILLS.RT_BILL_NO=M.RT_BILL_NO AND RT_BILL_NO IS NOT NULL )) ;
COMMIT;
NULL;
--##-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------##--
Update IAS_POS_RT_BILL_MST SET PAYED =2
SELECT RT_BILL_NO FROM IAS_POS_RT_BILL_MST M WHERE PAYED=0
AND EXISTS (SELECT RT_BILL_NO FROM IAS_POS_PAY_CASH WHERE IAS_POS_PAY_CASH.RT_BILL_NO=M.RT_BILL_NO AND RT_BILL_NO IS NOT NULL )) ;
END UPDATE_RT_BILL_PAY_RPLC_SRVR;

JOB_TYPE => 'STORED_PROCEDURE',
JOB_ACTION => 'UPDATE_RT_BILL_PAY
_RPLC_SRVR',
START_DATE => SYSTIMESTAMP,

END_DATE => NULL ,
COMMENTS => 'Refresh Data',
ENABLED => TRUE);

CREATE OR REPLACE Package Ias_Pos_Inv_Pkg Is
Procedure Insrt_Detail_Inv_Sub_To_Main (P_Yr_Br In Varchar2,P_Db_Link In Varchar2);
Procedure Insrt_Machine_Dtl_Sub_To_Main(P_Yr_Br In Varchar2,P_Db_Link In Varchar2);
