CREATE OR REPLACE Package Body Ias_Pos_Inv_Pkg Is
Procedure Insrt_Detail_Inv_Sub_To_Main(P_Yr_Br In Varchar2,P_Db_Link In Varchar2) Is
Cursor C_Master_Inv_Mst Is Select Inv_Ser,Inv_No, Inv_Type ,Inv_Date, Inv_Desc,
Ref_No, Annual, Ad_U_Id,
Up_Cnt, Brn_No, Brn_Year,
Cmp_No,Brn_Usr,
Use_Attach_Inv, External_Post,
Processed,Machine_No
From Master_Inv
Where Exists(Select 1 From Ias_Master_Inv_Tmp
Where Ias_Master_Inv_Tmp.Inv_Ser = Master_Inv.Inv_Ser)
Order By Inv_Type , Inv_No ;
V_Inv_No Master_Inv.Inv_No%Type ;
V_Inv_Serial Ias_Para_Inv.Man_Inv_Serail%Type ;
V_Inv_Ser Master_Inv.Inv_Ser%Type ;
V_External_Post NUMBER;
IF P_Yr_Br=USER THEN
V_External_Post:=0;
V_External_Post:=1;
For I In C_Master_Inv_Mst Loop
Execute Immediate ' Select 1 From '||p_yr_br||'.Master_Inv@'||P_Db_Link||'
Where Inv_Ser ='|| I.Inv_Ser
Raise_Application_Error(-20001,'Error When Read Inv Data From Headoffice'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
Select Man_Inv_Serail
Into V_Inv_Serial
From Ias_Para_Inv ;
V_Inv_Serial := 1 ;
If V_Inv_Serial = 1 Then
Execute Immediate ' Select Nvl(Max(Inv_No),0) + 1
From '||p_yr_br||'.Master_Inv@'||P_Db_Link
Into V_Inv_No ;
V_Inv_Ser := 1||Lpad(I.Brn_No,3,0)||V_Inv_No ;
ElsIf V_Inv_Serial = 2 Then
From '||p_yr_br||'.Master_Inv@'||P_Db_Link||' Where Inv_Type = '|| I.Inv_Type
V_Inv_Ser := 1||Lpad(I.Brn_No,3,0)||Lpad(I.Inv_Type,3,0)||V_Inv_No ;
V_Inv_No := I.Inv_No ;
V_Inv_Ser := I.Inv_Ser ;
Execute Immediate 'Insert Into '||p_yr_br||'.Master_Inv@'||P_Db_Link||'(
Inv_Type,Inv_No,Inv_Ser,Inv_Date, Inv_Desc,
Cmp_No, Brn_Usr,
Processed,Machine_No)
Select Inv_Type ,'||V_Inv_No||', '||V_Inv_Ser||',Inv_Date, Inv_Desc||'||I.Inv_no||',
Use_Attach_Inv, '||V_External_Post||',
Where Inv_No = '||I.Inv_No ||'
And Inv_Type = '||I.Inv_Type ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20002,'Error When Insert Into Master_Inv'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
Execute Immediate 'Insert Into '||p_yr_br||'.Detail_Inv@'||P_Db_Link||'(
Inv_Type,Inv_No,Inv_Ser,I_Code, Itm_Unt,
W_Code, I_Qty, P_Size,
P_Qty, B_Code, Expire_Date,
Batch_No, Avl_Qty, Rcrd_No,
Use_Attch, Brn_No, Brn_Year,
Item_Desc, Attch_No1, Attch_Desc_No1,
Attch_No2, Attch_Desc_No2, Attch_No3,
Attch_Desc_No3, Attch_No4, Attch_Desc_No4,
Attch_No5, Attch_Desc_No5, Flex_No)
Select Inv_Type,'||V_Inv_No||','||V_Inv_Ser||', I_Code, Itm_Unt,
Attch_No5, Attch_Desc_No5, Flex_No
From Detail_Inv
Where Inv_No = '||I.Inv_No||'
Raise_Application_Error(-20003,'Error When Insert Into Detail_Inv'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
Update Master_Inv
Where Ias_Master_Inv_Tmp.Inv_Ser = Master_Inv.Inv_Ser) ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20004,'Error When Update External_Post In Master_Inv'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
End Insrt_Detail_Inv_Sub_To_Main ;
Procedure Insrt_Machine_Dtl_Sub_To_Main(P_Yr_Br In Varchar2,P_Db_Link In Varchar2) Is
Cursor C_Inv_Machine_Mst Is Select Inv_Ser,Inv_No, Inv_Type ,Inv_Date, Inv_Desc,
Processed,Machine_No,Machine_Nm
From Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst
Where Exists(Select 1 From Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst_Tmp
Where Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst_Tmp.Inv_Ser = Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst.Inv_Ser)
Order By Inv_Type,Inv_No ;
V_Inv_No Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst.Inv_No%Type ;
V_Inv_Ser Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst.Inv_Ser%Type ;
For I In C_Inv_Machine_Mst Loop
Execute Immediate ' Select 1 From '||p_yr_br||'.Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst@'||P_Db_Link||'
From '||p_yr_br||'.Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst@'||P_Db_Link
V_Inv_Ser := I.Brn_Year||Lpad(I.Brn_No,6,0)||33||V_Inv_No ;
From '||p_yr_br||'.Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst@'||P_Db_Link||' Where Inv_Type = '||I.I
nv_Type
V_Inv_Ser := I.Brn_Year||Lpad(I.Brn_No,6,0)||33||Lpad(I.Inv_Type,5,0)||V_Inv_No ;
Execute Immediate 'Insert Into '||p_yr_br||'.Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst@'||P_Db_Link||'(
Inv_Type,Inv_No,Inv_Ser,Inv_Date,Inv_Desc,
Processed,Machine_No,Machine_Nm)
Use_Attach_Inv,'||V_External_Post||',
Raise_Application_Error(-20002,'Error When Insert Into Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
Execute Immediate 'Insert Into '||p_yr_br||'.Ias_Inv_Mac
hine_Dtl@'||P_Db_Link||'(
From Ias_Inv_Machine_Dtl
Raise_Application_Error(-20003,'Error When Insert Into Ias_Inv_Machine_Dtl'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
Update Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst
Where Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst_Tmp.Inv_Ser = Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst.Inv_Ser) ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20004,'Error When Update External_Post In Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
End Insrt_Machine_Dtl_Sub_To_Main ;
CREATE OR REPLACE Package Ias_Doc_Serial_Pkg As
FUNCTION Man_Inv_Serial( P_Brn_No In S_Brn.Brn_No%Type ,
P_Ser_Type In Ias_Para_Inv.Man_Inv_Serail%Type ,
P_Inv_Type In Master_Inv.Inv_Type%Type ) Return Number ;
--##-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------##--
FUNCTION Machine_Man_Inv_Serial( P_Brn_No In S_Brn.Brn_No%Type ,
P_Inv_Type In Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst.Inv_Type%Type ) Return Number ;
FUNCTION Request_Out_Trnsfr_Serial(P_Brn_No In S_Brn.Brn_No%Type ,
P_Ser_Type In Ias_Para_Inv.Request_Serial%Type ,
P_Req_Type In Ias_Out_Request_Mst.Out_Req_Type%Type ,
P_W_Code In Ias_Out_Request_Mst.W_Code%Type ) Return Number ;
End Ias_Doc_Serial_Pkg;
CREATE OR REPLACE Package Body Ias_Doc_Serial_Pkg As
P_Inv_Type In Master_Inv.Inv_Type%Type ) Return Number Is
If P_Ser_Type = 1 Then
Select Nvl(Max(Inv_No),0)+1
Into V_Inv_No
Where Brn_No = P_Brn_No ;
V_Inv_No := Null ;
ElsIf P_Ser_Type = 2 Then
Where Brn_No = P_Brn_No
And Inv_Type = P_Inv_Type ;
Return(V_Inv_No);
End Man_Inv_Serial ;
P_Inv_Type In Ias_Inv_Machine_Mst.Inv_Type%Type ) Return Number Is
End Machine_Man_Inv_Serial ;
P_W_Code In Ias_Out_Request_Mst.W_Code%Type ) Return Number Is
V_Out_Req_No Ias_Out_Request_Mst.Out_Req_No%Type ;
If P_Ser_Type = 1 Then -- Accumulated
Select Nvl(Max(Out_Req_No),0)+1
Into V_Out_Req_No
From Ias_Out_Request_Mst
V_Out_Req_No := Null ;
ElsIf P_Ser_Type = 2 Then -- By Type
And Out_Req_Type = P_Req_Type ;
ElsIf P_Ser_Type = 3 Then -- By Warehouse
And W_Code In
(Select W_Code From Warehouse_Details
Where Nvl(W_Ser,0)=( Select Nvl(W_Ser,0)
Where W_Code = P_W_Code) ) ;
ElsIf P_Ser_Type = 4 Then -- By Warehouse & Type
Return(V_Out_Req_No);
End Request_Out_Trnsfr_Serial ;
End Ias_Doc_Serial_Pkg ;

CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY IAS_AUD_SYS_PKG AS
--==============================================
PROCEDURE GET_COL_PK ( P_TAB_NM IN VARCHAR2 , V_COL_NM1 OUT VARCHAR2, V_COL_NM2 OUT VARCHAR2,V_COL_NM3 OUT VARCHAR2 ) IS
v_one number:=0;
cursor pk_cv Is
Select B.Column_Name
From User_Constraints A, User_Cons_Columns B
Where A.Owner=B.Owner
And A.Constraint_Name=B.Constraint_Name
And A.Table_Name=P_Tab_Nm
And Constraint_Type = 'P';
For i in pk_cv loop
If v_one = 0 Then
V_COL_NM1:=i.Column_Name;
If v_one = 1 Then
V_COL_NM2:=i.Column_Name;
If v_one = 2 Then
V_COL_NM3:=i.Column_Name;
v_one:=v_one + 1;
END Get_Col_Pk;
FUNCTION Get_Aud_Cols_Fnc ( P_Tab In Varchar2) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
V_Sql varchar2(32000);
Cursor Tb Is Select Table_name,column_name From Tab,User_Tab_Cols --User_Tables
Where Tab.Tname = User_Tab_Cols.Table_name
and Table_name = P_Tab
and column_name Not In ('AD_DATE','AD_U_ID','UP_DATE','UP_U_ID',
'CMP_NO','BRN_USR','BRN_NO','BRN_YEAR',
'I_CODE','P_SIZE','P_QTY','PF_QTY','TRMNL_LGN',
'LOGIN','LOGIN_CNT','LOGGED_ON','LOGOUT','OPEN_BAL',
'OPEN_FBAL','CURR_BAL','CURR_FBAL','OPEN_BAL_L','OPEN_BAL_F',
'CURR_BAL_L','CURR_BAL_F','I_CWTAVG','ITEM_STORE','INCOME_DATE',
'PROCESSED','PO_PROCESSED','PROCESED','STK_PROCESSED','JV_PROCESSED','TD_PROCESSED',
'LAST_UPDATE','LAST_DATE_LGN','MAX_POST_DATE')
Order by Table_name,column_name,column_id;
v_col_nm1 varchar2(30);
v_col_nm2 varchar2(30);
v_col_nm3 varchar2(30);
v_col_nm_1 varchar2(30);
v_col_nm_2 varchar2(30);
v_col_nm_3 varchar2(30);
v_cols varchar2(3000);
For i in Tb Loop
IAS_AUD_SYS_PKG.Get_Col_Pk(i.Table_name,v_col_nm1,v_col_nm2,v_col_nm3);
If v_col_nm1 is not null Then
v_col_nm_1:=':NEW.'||v_col_nm1;
v_col_nm_1:='NULL';
If v_col_nm2 is not null Then
v_col_nm_2:=':NEW.'||v_col_nm2;
v_col_nm_2:='NULL';
If v_col_nm3 is not null Then
v_col_nm_3:=':NEW.'||v_col_nm3;
v_col_nm_3:='NULL';
--=================================
IF IAS_DBS_Sys_Pkg.Check_Object ( i.Table_name , 'TABLE' , 'CMP_NO' ) > 0 THEN
v_cols:=',NVL(:NEW.CMP_NO,:OLD.CMP_NO)';
v_cols:=',NULL';
IF IAS_DBS_Sys_Pkg.Check_Object ( i.Table_name , 'TABLE' , 'BRN_NO' ) > 0 THEN
v_cols:= v_cols||',NVL(:NEW.BRN_NO,:OLD.BRN_NO)';
v_cols:=v_cols||',NULL';
IF IAS_DBS_Sys_Pkg.Check_Object ( i.Table_name , 'TABLE' , 'BRN_USR' ) > 0 THEN
v_cols:= v_cols||',NVL(:NEW.BRN_USR,:OLD.BRN_USR)';
IF IAS_DBS_Sys_Pkg.Check_Object ( i.Table_name , 'TABLE' , 'BRN_YEAR' ) > 0 THEN
v_cols:= v_cols||',NVL(:NEW.BRN_YEAR,:OLD.BRN_YEAR)';
V_Sql:= V_Sql||'
IAS_AUD_SYS_PKG.ADD_HIST( V_OP_TYPE,'''||i.Table_name||''','''||i.column_name||''',:NEW.'||i.column_name||',:OLD.'||i.column_name||','||v_col_nm_1||','||v_col_nm_2||','||v_col_nm_3||''||v_cols||');' ;
Return(V_Sql);
END Get_Aud_Cols_Fnc;
FUNCTION Get_Aud_Cols_Fnc2 ( P_Tab In Varchar2) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
IAS_AUD_SYS_PKG.ADD_HIST( V_OP_TYPE,'''||i.Table_name||''','''||i.column_name||''',:NEW.'||i.column_name||',:OLD.'||i.column_name||','||v_col_nm_1||','||v_col_nm_2||','||v_col_nm_3||');' ;
END Get_Aud_Cols_Fnc2;
P_AUD_ST IN NUMBER DEFAULT 1) Is
V_USER_NO:=P_USER_NO;

End SET_PARA;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION Ias_Get_Doc_Del_Fnc ( P_Brn_No In S_Brn.Brn_No%Type ,
P_Serial_Type In Number ,
P_Doc_Ser In Number ,
P_Doc_Type In Number ,
P_doc_jv_type In Number Default Null ,
P_Pur_Type In Number Default Null ,
P_Cc_Code In Varchar2 Default Null ,
P_w_code In Number Default Null ,
P_Cash_No In Number Default Null ) RETURN NUMBER IS
V_Doc_No Number:= Null;
V_Cnt Number:= 0;
P_Whr Varchar2(20
00);
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1- accumulated
-- 2- doc_jv_type
-- 3- by cost_center
-- 4- warehouse
-- 5- warehouse + doc_jv_type
-- 6- by cost_center + doc_jv_type
-- 7- by cash
-- 8- by bank
-- 9- By Journal
V_Cnt := Ias_gen_Pkg.Get_Cnt('Select 1 From Ias_Doc_Del
Where Brn_No = '||P_Brn_No||'
And Doc_Type = '||P_Doc_Type||'
And Doc_Ser = '||P_Doc_Ser ||'
And RowNum<=1');
If P_Serial_Type=2 Then
P_Whr := ' And doc_jv_type='||P_doc_jv_type;
ElsIf P_Serial_Type=3 Then
P_Whr := ' And Cc_Code In ( Select Cc_Code From Cost_Centers
Where Nvl(C_Sr,0)=(Select Nvl(C_Sr,0)
From Cost_Centers
where Cc_Code='''||P_Cc_code||'''))';
ElsIf P_Serial_Type = 4 Then
P_Whr := ' And W_Code In ( Select W_Code From Warehouse_Details
Where Nvl(W_Ser,0)=(Select Nvl(W_Ser,0)
where W_Code='||P_w_code||'))';
ElsIf P_Serial_Type=5 Then
P_Whr := ' And w_code='||P_w_code||
' And doc_jv_type='||P_doc_jv_type;
ElsIf P_Serial_Type=6 Then
P_Whr := ' And cc_code='''||P_cc_code||'''
And doc_jv_type='||P_doc_jv_type;
ElsIf P_Serial_Type=7 Then
P_Whr := ' And doc_jv_type='||P_doc_jv_type||'
And Cash_no In ( Select Cash_no From cash_in_hand
Where Nvl(cash_sr,0)=(Select Nvl(cash_sr,0)
where Cash_no='||P_Cash_no||'))';
ElsIf P_Serial_Type=8 Then
And Cash_no In ( Select bank_no From cash_at_bank
Where Nvl(bank_sr,0)=(Select Nvl(bank_sr,0)
where bank_no='||P_Cash_no||'))';
ElsIf P_Serial_Type= 10 Then
P_Whr := ' And pur_type= '||P_Pur_Type;
V_Doc_No := Ias_gen_Pkg.Get_Cnt('Select Nvl(Max(Doc_No),0)+1 From Ias_Doc_Del
And Doc_Type ='||P_Doc_Type||P_Whr);
Return(V_Doc_No);
END Ias_Get_Doc_Del_Fnc;
CREATE OR REPLACE Package Ias_Pos_Request_Pkg Is
Procedure Insrt_Out_Request_Sub_To_Main(P_Yr_Br In Varchar2);
End Ias_Pos_Request_Pkg;
CREATE OR REPLACE Package Body Ias_Pos_Request_Pkg Is
Procedure Insrt_Out_Request_Sub_To_Main(P_Yr_Br In Varchar2) Is
Cursor C_Req_Out_Tr_Mst Is Select Out_Req_Type, Out_Req_No, Out_Req_Ser,
Out_Req_Date, W_Code,F_W_Code,Req_Side,
Doc_Desc, Processed, Inactive,
Approved, Ref_No, Aprv_U_Id,
Aprv_Date, Ad_U_Id, Ad_Date,
Up_U_Id, Up_Date, Up_Cnt,
External_Post, Brn_No, Brn_Year,
HRCHY_NO, Pj_No, Cc_Code, Reserve_No,AD_TRMNL_NM,UP_TRMNL_NM
Where Exists(Select 1 From IAS_POS_OUT_REQUEST_MST_TMP
Where IAS_POS_OUT_REQUEST_MST_TMP.Out_Req_Ser = Ias_Out_Request_Mst.Out_Req_Ser
Order By Out_Req_Ser ;
Cnt Number := 0 ;
V_Out_Req_Ser Ias_Out_Request_Mst.Out_Req_Ser%Type ;
V_Req_Ser Ias_Para_Inv.Request_Serial%Type ;
For I In C_Req_Out_Tr_Mst Loop
Execute Immediate ' Select 1 From '||p_yr_br||'.Ias_Out_Request_Mst@onyx.onyx.com
Where Out_Req_Ser ='|| I.Out_Req_Ser
Into Cnt ;
Cnt := 0 ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20001,'Error When Read Out Request Data From Headoffice'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
If Cnt > 0 Then
Select Request_Serial
Into V_Req_Ser
From Ias_Para_Inv
V_Req_Ser := 1 ;
If nvl(V_Req_Ser,1) = 1 Then
Execute Immediate ' Select Nvl(Max(Out_Req_No),0) + 1
From '||p_yr_br||'.Ias_Out_Request_Mst@onyx.onyx.com
Where Brn_No = '||I.Brn_no
Into V_Out_Req_No;
ElsIf V_Req_Ser = 2 Then
Where Out_Req_Type = '||I.Out_Req_Type||'
and Brn_No = '||I.Brn_no
Into V_Out_Req_No ;
ElsIf V_Req_Ser = 3 Then
Where w_code = '||I.w_code||'
ElsIf V_Req_Ser = 4 Then
and w_code = '||I.w_code||'
If nvl(V_Req_Ser,1)=1 Then
V_Out_Req_Ser := I.Brn_Year||Lpad(I.BRN_NO,6,0)||V_Out_Req_No ;
elsif V_Req_Ser=2 Then
V_Out_Req_Ser := I.Brn_Year||Lpad(I.BRN_NO,6,0)||Lpad(I.Out_Req_Type,5,0)||V_Out_Req_No ;
elsif V_Req_Ser=3 Then
V_Out_Req_Ser := I.Brn_Year||Lpad(I.BRN_NO,6,0)||Lpad(I.w_code,6,0)||V_Out_Req_No ;
elsif V_Req_Ser=4 Then
V_Out_Req_Ser := I.Brn_Year||Lpad(I.BRN_NO,6,0)||Lpad(I.w_code,6,0)||Lpad(I.Out_Req_Type,5,0)||V_Out_Req_No ;
end if;
V_Out_Req_No := I.Out_Req_No ;
V_Out_Req_Ser := I.Out_Req_Ser ;
Execute Immediate 'Insert Into '||p_yr_br||'.Ias_Out_Request_Mst@onyx.onyx.com(
Out_Req_Type, Out_Req_No, Out_Req_Ser,
Out_Req_Date, W_Code,F_W_Code, Req_Side,
HRCHY_NO, Pj_No, Cc_Code, Reserve_No,AD_TRMNL_NM,UP_TRMNL_NM)
Select Out_Req_Type,'||V_Out_Req_No||','||V_Out_Req_Ser||',
Doc_Desc||'||I.Out_Req_No||',
Processed, Inactive,
1, Brn_No, Brn_Year,
Where Out_Req_Ser = '||I.Out_Req_Ser ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20002,'Error When Insert Into Ias_Out_Request_Mst'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
Execute Immediate 'Insert Into '||p_yr_br||'.Ias_Out_Request_Dtl@onyx.onyx.com(
I_Code, Itm_Unt, I_Qty,
P_Size, P_Qty, W_Code, F_W_Code ,
Item_Desc, Rcrd_No, External_Post,
Use_Attch, Rec_Attch, Brn_No,
Brn_Year, Cmp_No,
Brn_Usr, Doc_Type_Ref, Doc_No_Ref,
Doc_Ser_Ref, Reserved,Res_Date, Avl_Qty,
EXPIRE_DATE, BATCH_NO,WT_UNT, WT_QTY,
I_LENGTH,I_WIDTH,I_HEIGHT,I_NUMBER,F_AVL_QTY,
BARCODE,CC_CODE, PJ_NO, ACTV_NO )
P_Size, P_Qty, W_Code,F_W_Code,
Brn_Usr, Doc
_Type_Ref, Doc_No_Ref,
BARCODE,CC_CODE, PJ_NO, ACTV_NO
From Ias_Out_Request_Dtl
Raise_Application_Error(-20003,'Error When Insert Into Ias_Out_Request_Dtl'||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
Execute Immediate 'Insert Into '||p_yr_br||'.S_APPRVD_MOV@onyx.onyx.com(
LVL_NO, DOC_TYP, DOC_SER, DOC_NO, DOC_JV_TYP, DOC_DATE, APPRVD,
APPRVD_U_ID, APPRVD_DATE, APPRVD_DESC, ORDR_NO, CMP_NO, BRN_NO,
BRN_YEAR, BRN_USR, AD_U_ID, AD_DATE, UP_U_ID, UP_DATE, AD_TRMNL_NM, UP_TRMNL_NM)
Select
LVL_NO, DOC_TYP, '||V_Out_Req_Ser||','||V_Out_Req_No||', DOC_JV_TYP, DOC_DATE, APPRVD,
BRN_YEAR, BRN_USR, AD_U_ID, AD_DATE, UP_U_ID, UP_DATE, AD_TRMNL_NM, UP_TRMNL_NM
From S_APPRVD_MOV
Where DOC_SER = '||I.Out_Req_Ser ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20002,'Error When Insert Into S_APPRVD_MOV '||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
Update Ias_Out_Request_Mst
and RowNum<=1) ;
Raise_Application_Error(-20004,'Error When Update Processed In Ias_Out_Request_Mst '||Chr(13)||SqlErrm);
End Insrt_Out_Request_Sub_To_Main;
End Ias_Pos_Request_Pkg ;
EXECUTE IMMEDIATE ' Update '||USER||'.IAS_POS_RT_BILL_MST@ONYX.ONYX.COM SET PAYED =1
select rt_bill_no from '||USER||'.IAS_POS_RT_BILL_MST@ONYX.ONYX.COM M WHERE PAYED=0
AND EXISTS (SELECT RT_BILL_NO FROM IAS_POS_PAY_BILLS WHERE IAS_POS_PAY_BILLS.RT_BILL_NO=M.RT_BILL_NO AND RT_BILL_NO IS NOT NULL )) ';
-- COMMIT;
EXECUTE IMMEDIATE ' Update '||User||'.IAS_POS_RT_BILL_MST@ONYX.ONYX.COM SET PAYED =2
SELECT RT_BILL_NO FROM '||USER||'.IAS_POS_RT_BILL_MST@ONYX.ONYX.COM M WHERE PAYED=0
AND EXISTS (SELECT RT_BILL_NO FROM IAS_POS_PAY_CASH WHERE IAS_POS_PAY_CASH.RT_BILL_NO=M.RT_BILL_NO AND RT_BILL_NO IS NOT NULL )) ';

RVD_PKG AS
FUNCTION GET_LVL_NM(P_DOC_TYP IN S_APPRVD_LVL.DOC_TYP%TYPE,P_LVL_NO IN S_APPRVD_LVL.LVL_NO%TYPE)RETURN VARCHAR2 ;

Raise_Application_Error(-20002,'Err When Insert Into S_Apprvd_Mov = '||Sqlerrm);
END INSRT_APPRVD_LVL;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION Doc_Type_Name(P_lang In NUMBER,
P_doctype In NUMBER,
P_jvtype In NUMBER Default Null) RETURN CHAR Is
v_jvname VARCHAR2(100);
v_dvname VARCHAR2(50);
If P_doctype = 0 Then
Return (Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt (P_lang,1647));
ElsIf P_jvtype Is Null Then
Select FLG_DESC
Into v_jvname
From S_Flags
Where Lang_no = P_lang
and Flg_Code ='DOC_TYPE'
and Flg_Value= P_doctype;
Return(v_jvname);
Return(P_doctype);
ElsIf P_jvtype Is Not Null Then
Select Flg_Desc
Into v_dvname
v_dvname:=Null;
If P_Doctype In (2,3) Then
and Flg_Code ='CASH_CHQ'
and Flg_Value= P_jvtype;
v_jvname:=Null;
ElsIf P_Doctype In (4,5,6,7) Then
and Flg_Code ='TYPE_NAME_SI'
ElsIf P_Doctype = 10 Then
and Flg_Code ='STK_ADJ_TYPE'
ElsIf P_Doctype >=60 Then
Select jv_name
From Ias_Sys.IAS_DOCJV_TYPE_SYSTEMS
and DOC_TYPE = P_Doctype
and JV_TYPE = P_jvtype
and Lang_no = P_lang;
Return(v_dvname||' '||v_jvname);
Return(P_Doctype);
END Doc_Type_Name;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION Get_Icode_AvlQty_Priv ( P_Icode In VARCHAR2,
P_Psize In NUMBER,
P_Wcode In WAREHOUSE_DETAILS.W_CODE%TYPE DEFAULT NUll,
P_UserNo In Number )
RETURN NUMBER
avlq NUMBER:=0;
--=======================================================
If P_Wcode Is Not Null Then --(1)
Select nvl(Sum(distinct Avl_qty),0)/P_Psize
From Ias_Itm_Wcode,Privilege_wh
Where I_Code=P_Icode
and U_id=P_UserNo
and Ias_Itm_Wcode.w_code=Privilege_wh.w_code
and Ias_Itm_Wcode.W_code=P_Wcode
and nvl(add_flag,0)=1;
WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
avlq:=0;
WHEN Others THEN
Else --(1)
Select Sum(avlq)/P_Psize Into avlq From (
Select Sum(distinct Avl_qty) avlq,Ias_Itm_Wcode.w_code
and nvl(add_flag,0)=1
Group By Ias_Itm_Wcode.w_code );
RETURN (nvl(avlq,0));
END Get_Icode_AvlQty_Priv;
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION Get_Items_ACtivity( P_Activity_No In Ias_Items_Activity_Priv.Activity_No%TYPE ,
P_User_No In User_R.U_Id%Type ,
P_Conn_Itm_Act In Ias_Para_Inv.Conn_Itm_Act_By_Usr_Priv%Type,
P_Op_type In Number Default 1) RETURN NUMBER IS
Cnt NUMBER ;
If Nvl(P_Conn_Itm_Act,0) = 1 Then
From Ias_Items_Activity_Priv
Where Ias_Items_Activity_Priv.Activity_No = P_Activity_No
And U_Id = P_User_No
And Decode(P_Op_type,1,Add_Flag,View_Flag) = 1
Cnt := 1 ;
End Get_Items_ACtivity;

P_Brn_Scr In Number ,
P_Brn_Data In Number ,
P_Trns_Type In Number ) RETURN NUMBER IS
If P_Brn_Scr = P_Brn_Data Then --P_Trns_Type (1-Update , 2-Delete)
END Ias_Chk_Brn_Up_Del_Fnc;
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION Get_Icode_AvlQty ( P_Icode In IAS_ITM_MST.I_CODE%TYPE,
p_Psize In NUMBER,
p_Wcode In WAREHOUSE_DETAILS.W_CODE%TYPE DEFAULT NUll,
p_ExpDate In DATE DEFAULT NULL,
p_BatchNo In VARCHAR2 DEFAULT NULL,
p_idate In DATE DEFAULT NULL)
If p_Wcode Is Not Null Then
Select nvl(Sum(distinct Avl_Qty),0)/p_Psize
and Ias_Itm_Wcode.W_code=p_Wcode;
Select Sum(avlq) Into avlq From (
Select (Sum(distinct Avl_Qty)/nvl( P_Psize,1)) avlq,w_code
Where Ias_Itm_Wcode.I_code=P_Icode
Group By w_code );
END Get_Icode_AvlQty;
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION Get_Icode_RealQty (P_Icode In IAS_ITM_MST.I_CODE%TYPE,
p_Wcode In WAREHOUSE_DETAILS.W_CODE%TYPE DEFAULT NUll)
V_Avl_Qty NUMBER:=0;
V_Pos_Qty NUMBER:=0;
V_Pos_Rt_Qty NUMBER:=0;
V_Real_Qty NUMBER:=0;
Select nvl(Sum(distinct avl_qty),0)/P_Psize
Into V_Avl_Qty
and Ias_Itm_Wcode.W_Code=Nvl(P_Wcode,Ias_Itm_Wcode.W_Code);
V_Avl_Qty:=0;
--##Get Bill Qty
Select (Sum(Nvl(P_qty,0))+Sum(Nvl(Free_Qty,0)*Nvl(P_Size,1))) /P_Psize
Into V_Pos_Qty
From ias_pos_Bill_Mst m,Ias_pos_Bill_Dtl d
Where m.bill_no=d.bill_no
and I_Code=P_Icode
and d.W_Code=Nvl(P_Wcode,D.W_Code)
and Nvl(POSTED,0)=0;
V_Pos_Qty:=0;
--##Get Rt Bill Qty
Into V_Pos_Rt_Qty
From Ias_pos_Rt_Bill_Mst m,Ias_pos_Rt_Bill_Dtl d
Where m.Rt_Bill_no=d.Rt_Bill_no
V_Pos_Rt_Qty:=0;
V_Real_Qty :=Nvl(V_Avl_Qty,0)-(Nvl(V_Pos_Qty,0)+Nvl(V_Pos_Rt_Qty,0));
Return (nvl(V_Real_Qty,0));
END Get_Icode_RealQty;
CREATE OR REPLACE Function Get_Pos_Net_Sale_Qty (P_I_C
ode In Ias_Itm_Mst.I_Code%Type,
P_P_Size In Number Default 1,
P_F_Date In Date Default Null,
P_T_Date In Date Default Null,
P_Exp_Date In Date Default Null ,
P_Batch_No In Varchar2 Default Null ,
P_Type In Number Default 1)
Return Number
V_Net_Qty Number:=0;
V_Pos_Bill_Qty Number:=0;
V_Pos_Rt_Qty Number:=0;
Select (Sum(Nvl(P_Qty,0))+Sum(Nvl(Free_Qty,0)*Nvl(P_Size,1))) /P_P_Size
Into V_Pos_Bill_Qty
From Ias_Pos_Bill_Mst M,Ias_Pos_Bill_Dtl D
Where M.Bill_No=D.Bill_No
And I_Code =P_I_Code
And D.W_Code = Decode(P_W_code,Null,D.W_Code,P_W_code)
And Expire_Date=Decode(P_Exp_Date,Null,Expire_Date,Nvl(P_Exp_Date,'01/01/1900'))
And Batch_No = Decode(P_Batch_No,Null,Batch_No,Nvl(P_Batch_No,'0'))
And M.Bill_Date Between To_Date(P_F_Date,'DD/MM/YYYY') And To_Date(P_T_Date,'DD/MM/YYYY')
And Nvl(Posted,0)=0;
V_Pos_Bill_Qty:=0;
From Ias_Pos_Rt_Bill_Mst M,Ias_Pos_Rt_Bill_Dtl D
Where M.Rt_Bill_No=D.Rt_Bill_No
And M.Rt_Bill_Date Between To_Date(P_F_Date,'DD/MM/YYYY') And To_Date(P_T_Date,'DD/MM/YYYY')
If Nvl(P_Type,1)=1 Then
V_Net_Qty:=Nvl(V_Pos_Bill_Qty,0)-Nvl(V_Pos_Rt_Qty,0);
Elsif P_Type=2 Then
V_Net_Qty:=Nvl(V_Pos_Bill_Qty,0);
Elsif P_Type=3 Then
V_Net_Qty:=Nvl(V_Pos_Rt_Qty,0);
Return (Nvl(V_Net_Qty,0));
Return 0;
End Get_Pos_Net_Sale_Qty;
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION Ias_Get_Itm_Barcode_Fnc ( P_Barcode_Type Number , P_Icode Varchar2 , P_Barcode Varchar2) RETURN Varchar2 IS
V_Barcode Ias_Itm_Unt_Barcode.Barcode%Type;
V_Icode Ias_Itm_Unt_Barcode.I_Code%Type;
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
If P_Barcode_Type=1 Then -- Barcode Same Item
V_Icode := P_Icode;
If V_Icode Is Not Null Then
If P_Barcode_Type=1 And Nvl(P_Barcode,'0')<>'0' Then
V_Barcode := P_Barcode ;
Select Barcode InTo V_Barcode From Ias_Itm_Unt_Barcode Where I_Code=V_Icode And RowNum<=1;
V_Barcode := Null;
Return(V_Barcode);
END Ias_Get_Itm_Barcode_Fnc;
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION Get_BlNC_Cst_Fnc( P_loc_cur In VARCHAR2,
P_c_code In CUSTOMER.C_CODE%TYPE,
P_acy In VARCHAR2,
P_fd
In DATE
Default Null,
P_td
In DATE Default Null,
P_bal_type In Number Default 0)
RETURN NUMBER IS
Bl_Amt Number:=0;
V_Fd Date;
V_Td Date;
If P_Fd Is Not Null Then
V_Fd:=P_Fd;
Elsif P_Fd Is Null And P_Td Is Not Null Then
V_Fd:=P_Td;
If P_Td Is Not Null Then
V_Td:=P_Td;
Elsif P_Fd Is Not Null And P_Td Is Null Then
V_Td:=P_Fd;
If V_Fd Is Not Null And V_Td Is Not Null Then
Select Decode(P_Acy,P_Loc_Cur,Nvl(Sum(Dr_Amt),0)-Nvl(Sum(Cr_Amt),0),Nvl(Sum(Dr_Amt_F),0)-Nvl(Sum(Cr_Amt_F),0))
Into Bl_Amt
From Ias_Post_Dtl
Where C_Code=P_C_Code
And A_Cy=P_Acy
And Doc_Date Between V_Fd And V_Td
And Nvl(Cc_Code,'0') = Decode(P_Cc_Code,Null,Nvl(Cc_Code,'0'),P_Cc_Code);
Return(Nvl(Bl_Amt,0));
End Get_Blnc_Cst_Fnc;
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE Ias_Get_Qut_Prm_Price_Prc ( P_Date In Date,
P_ICode In Varchar2,
P_Rate In Number,
P_Nof In Number,
P_Q_No Out Number,
P_Price Out Number) IS
Q_Cur Varchar2(9);
Rate Number;
Prc Number;
Select Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Quot_No,Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.I_Price InTo P_Q_No,P_Price
Where Ias_Qut_Prm_Mst.Quot_No = Ias_Qut_Prm_Dtl.Quot_No
And I_Code = P_ICode
If P_Q_No Is Not Null Then
Select Quot_Cur InTo Q_Cur
From Ias_Qut_Prm_M
Where Quot_No = P_Q_No
Rate := Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Cur_rate(Q_cur);
P_Price := (Nvl(P_Price,0) * Nvl(round((Rate/P_Rate),P_Nof),0));
NULL; --RAISE;
END Ias_Get_Qut_Prm_Price_Prc;
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE IAS_Get_SalesDisc_Prc ( P_DiscType In NUMBER,
P_Sales_Disc_Wcode In NUMBER,
P_Iprice In NUMBER Default 0,
P_Bill_Doc_Type In Number,
P_bill_i_qty In Number Default 0,
P_Icode In IAS_ITM_MST.I_CODE%TYPE,
P_Itm_Unt In IAS_ITM_DTL.ITM_UNT%TYPE,
P_Gcode In GROUP_DETAILS.G_CODE%TYPE,
P_BillDate In Date,
P_Cst_Class In NUMBER Default 0,
P_LevNo In IAS_ITEM_PRICE.LEV_NO%TYPE,
P_billcur In Varchar2,
P_billrate In NUMBER,
P_wcode In WAREHOUSE_DETAILS.W_CODE%TYPE,
P_whgcode In NUMBER,
P_dis_per Out NUMBER,
P_dis_amt Out NUMBER,
P_Batch_No In Sales_Disc.Batch_No%Type,
P_Brn_No In S_Brn.Brn_No%Type) IS
-- amt_type 0= Per , 1= amt
If ( P_DiscType Between 1 And 9 ) Or ( P_DiscType = 12 ) Then
Select Decode(amt_type,0,amt),
Decode(amt_type,0,(amt*P_Iprice)/100,Decode(a_cy,P_billcur,amt,(ac_rate*1/P_billrate)))
Into P_dis_per,P_dis_amt
From Sales_Disc
Where Nvl(G_Code,'0')=Decode(Nvl(G_Code,'0'),'0','0',P_Gcode)
and Nvl(I_Code,'0')=Decode(Nvl(I_code,'0'),'0','0',P_Icode)
and Nvl(Itm_Unt,'0')=Decode(Nvl(Itm_Unt,'0'),'0','0',P_Itm_Unt)
and Nvl(C_Code,'0')=Decode(Nvl(C_Code,'0'),'0','0',P_C_Code)
and Nvl(C_Class,0)=Decode(Nvl(C_Class,0),0,0,P_Cst_Class)
and Nvl(Lev_No,0)=Decode(Nvl(Lev_No,0),0,0,P_LevNo)
And Nvl(Batch_No,'0')=Decode(Nvl(Batch_No,'0'),'0','0',P_Batch_No)
and Decode(P_Sales_Disc_Wcode,2,w_code,3,whg_code,0)=Decode(P_Sales_Disc_Wcode,2,P_Wcode,3,P_whgcode,0)
and P_BillDate Between F_Date and T_Date;
ElsIf P_DiscType In (10,11) Then
and Nvl(P_Bill_I_Qty,0) Between NVL(F_Qty,0) And NVL(T_Qty,0)
and Nvl(Bill_Doc_Type,0)=Decode(Nvl(Bill_Doc_Type,0),0,0,P_Bill_Doc_Type)
and Decode(P_Sales_Disc_Wcode,2,w_code,3,whg_code,0)=Decode(P_Sa
les_Disc_Wcode,2,P_Wcode,3,P_whgcode,0)
ElsIf P_DiscType In (13,14 ) Then
and Nvl(Lev_No,0) = Decode(Nvl(Lev_No,0),0,0,P_LevNo)
P_dis_per:=0;
P_dis_amt:=0;
END IAS_Get_SalesDisc_Prc;
Form_no = P_Frm_No

Create Or Replace Force View Ias_V_Usr_Scr_Prv (
U_Id,
Usr_Name,
Form_No,
Form_Name,
Include_Flag,
Ad_Flag,
Del_Flag,
Mod_Flag,
View_Flag,
Print_Flag,
Vwrep_Flag,
Ad_U_Id,
Ad_Date,
Up_U_Id,
Up_Date,
Rec_Src,
F_Order_No,
Lang_No )
Select A.U_Id,
Decode ( Ias_Prmtr_Pkg.Getpval(P_Prmtr =>'LANG_NO') ,1,Nvl (B.U_A_Name, B.U_E_Name),Nvl (B.U_E_Name, B.U_A_Name)) Usr_Nm,
A.Form_No ,
C.Form_Name Form_Name ,
A.Include_Flag,
A.Ad_Flag,
A.Del_Flag,
A.Mod_Flag,
A.View_Flag,
A.Print_Flag,
A.Vwrep_Flag,
A.Ad_U_Id,
A.Ad_Date,
A.Up_U_Id,
A.Up_Date,
1 Rec_Src,
A.F_Order_No,
C.Lang_No
From Privilege A, User_R B, Ias_Form_Name C
Where A.U_Id = B.U_Id
And A.Form_No = C.Form_No
And Include_Flag = 1
Union
Select B.U_Id,
C.Form_No,
0 Include_Flag,
0 Ad_Flag,
0 Del_Flag,
0 Mod_Flag,
0 View_Flag,
0 Print_Flag,
0 Vwrep_Flag,
Null Ad_U_Id,
Null Ad_Date,
Null Up_U_Id,
Null Up_Date,
0 Rec_Src,
Null F_Order_No,
From User_R B, Ias_Form_Name C
Where
Not Exists
(Select 1
Where Form_No = C.Form_No And U_Id = B.U_Id And Rownum <= 1)

(U_ID, USR_NAME, MACHINE_NO, MACHINE_NAME, USED,
AD_U_ID, AD_DATE, UP_U_ID, UP_DATE)
Nvl (B.U_A_Name, B.U_E_Name) Usr_Nm,
A.MACHINE_NO,
TERMINAL machine_name,
A.USED,
A.Up_Date
From IAS_POS_PRIV_MACHINE A, User_R B, IAS_POS_MACHINE C
And A.MACHINE_NO = C.MACHINE_NO
C.MACHINE_NO,
0 USED,
Null Up_Date
From User_R B, IAS_POS_MACHINE C
Where Not Exists
From IAS_POS_PRIV_MACHINE
Where MACHINE_NO = C.MACHINE_NO And U_Id = B.U_Id And Rownum <= 1)
